  - **The frontend stops marking refused requests as running (`frontend/src/hooks/api/useSession.ts`, `frontend/src/components/AnalysisPanel.tsx`)**: `resumeAnalysis` and `continueRound` honour `success === false` and `data.runtime_unavailable` — four refusals reach them (runtime unavailable, recovery blocked, snapshot failure, pending plan review) and only the first carries a flag. A new shared `blockForwardResume` guards all three entry points that advance the analysis; previously only Resume checked, so the round dialog's Continue and Send sent a request the backend refused and then set `status: 'running'`, which hides the re-run button behind `!isRunning` and left a page reload as the only way back. The guard leaves the dialog mounted so the user's typed message and file selection survive. The snapshot retry poll now stops and corrects the capability flag on `SNAPSHOT_V2_DISABLED` instead of retrying a guaranteed 404 until the tab closes, and the runtime banner no longer points at a `/rerun` endpoint that does not exist.
  - **Startup says when it steps over restore journals (`backend/main.py`)**: with V2 disabled, `_warn_about_unrecovered_journals` reports sessions holding a `STAGED`/`STAGING`/`APPLYING` journal (or an unreadable one). A crash during the APPLYING stage leaves the tree half-swapped and the journal describing how to finish or roll back; recovery genuinely cannot run without the object store, but the operator should learn it from a log line rather than from the results. Terminal journals are excluded — they are merely waiting for the next enabled startup to reap them.
  - **New tests (`backend/tests/test_export_import_hardening.py`, `backend/tests/test_export_import_routes_hardening.py`, `backend/tests/test_snapshot_v2_feature_gate.py`, `backend/tests/test_e2e_http_roundtrip.py`, `frontend/src/hooks/__tests__/useApi.resume-gate.test.ts`)**: `states/` rebinding and its boundary conditions, checkpoint dropping and retention, snapshot-pkl runtime marking, the armed restore intent, json-import id allocation, imported status normalization, recovery-block release without status damage, the checkpoint-plus-kernel test, and the resume-refusal handling. `test_e2e_http_roundtrip.py` drives the whole round trip — create, populate, export, import, continue — through the real routes and auth for four export/import combinations; it is what surfaced the throttled pre-export save that no unit-level check could reach.

- `0.36.9` — Session export/import hardening: import path confinement, session-id collision, portable-archive resumability, export authentication, link-member archives, endpoint/credential pairing, anchored path remapping, failure cleanup; version bump (2026-07-29):
  - **Import destinations confined to the session directory (`backend/routes/export_import_parts/import_impl.py`)**: `_resolve_import_dest` is now a whitelist that returns `None` for any archive path outside the session-centric layout, and the caller skips those members; the destination guard tightened from `validate_path_within(dest, DATA_BASE_DIR)` to `validate_path_within(dest, get_session_dir(session_id))`. The old catch-all (`return DATA_BASE_DIR / relative_to_base`) let any authenticated user place an archive member anywhere under the data root — `app.db` (the account and API-key store), `.jwt_secret` (the token signing key) and other users' `sessions/<id>/session.json` were all writable, and the write happened before the "session state JSON not found" check, so even a rejected import completed the damage. The product's own reproducibility bundle proved the branch was live code: it is emitted at the archive root and landed in a global `<data>/reproducibility/` shared by every import. `logs/` and `reproducibility/` now map into the session; the bundle goes to the workspace where the generator expects it.
  - **Import creates a session, never overwrites one (`backend/routes/export_import_parts/import_impl.py`)**: New `_allocate_import_session_id` / `_claim_session_id` take the archive's id only when free, claiming it atomically with `mkdir(exist_ok=False)`; on collision the archive is materialized under a fresh uuid and every embedded path is rebound (`_remap_paths_in_text` rewrites the session-directory prefix, `_rebind_session_id_values` rewrites standalone id values, `state_data["id"]` is reset). Previously the destination came entirely from the archive and nothing checked whether that session already existed — so re-importing a backup on the same installation overwrote the live session's `session.json` while leaving newer workspace files orphaned, and `state_data["user_id"] = user_id` handed another user's session to whoever imported it.
  - **Runtime availability reads what this import wrote (`backend/routes/export_import_parts/import_impl.py`)**: `_mark_runtime_availability_after_import` gained an `imported_pkl` argument. It previously answered "is there a pkl on disk", so a pkl belonging to an earlier run at the destination made an imported session look resumable against a kernel that does not match its state.
  - **Imported sessions can be run again (`backend/routes/lifecycle.py`, `backend/models/schemas.py`, `backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`, `backend/workflow/agents.py`)**: `POST /run` now sets `restore_start_intent="explicit_start"` when the session has no checkpoint and has already run, which routes `resume()` through `_invoke_restore_entry` instead of its "never invent a fresh initialize_round run" fall-through — that fall-through logged, flipped the status back to `PAUSED` and executed nothing, so Start/Re-run on an imported session was a silent no-op with no way forward at all. New one-shot `AnalysisState.runtime_recompute_pending`, set when the run is clearing an `unavailable` runtime, makes `_build_data_context` tell the agent its kernel is empty and to reload the input files; the bioinformatics node clears it after the first run.
  - **Session archives require authentication (`backend/middleware/auth.py`, `backend/routes/export_import_parts/export_routes.py`, `backend/routes/export_import_parts/core.py`, `frontend/src/components/Sidebar.tsx`)**: `/export` and `/export/prepare` are no longer auth-optional; only `/export/download` is, and it now requires an unguessable one-shot token (`secrets.token_urlsafe(32)`) that is bound to the session it was minted for and is not consumed by a wrong-session request. `require_session_ownership` degrades to an existence check when no token is presented, so listing `/export` made a whole-session archive — every upload, figure, report and message — downloadable by anyone who learned the session id from a deliberately auth-optional report URL. The `v1-<session_id>` prepared-export key was derivable from the same id.
  - **Export emits no link members (`backend/routes/export_import_parts/tar_builders.py`, `export_routes.py`)**: archives are built with `dereference=True` and each member is created from an already-open descriptor. Import rejects every SYMTYPE/LNKTYPE member as its first check, so a single `os.symlink` or `cp -l` left in a workspace by analysis code produced an archive that could never be imported by ScAI at all.
  - **Export tolerates a session that is still running (`backend/routes/export_import_parts/tar_builders.py`, `backend/utils/sandbox_parts/shared.py`)**: new `_safe_add_file` skips a file that vanishes or cannot be read instead of aborting the export with a 500, and `_PaddedReader` serves exactly the announced size so a file that shrinks mid-pack cannot raise inside `copyfileobj` and leave a structurally broken archive. The live notebook — rewritten in place on every save and packed into every archive — is now written atomically via a temp file plus `os.replace`.
  - **Endpoints never borrow the installation credential (`backend/utils/secret_redaction.py`, `backend/utils/settings_provider.py`, `backend/utils/agent_llm.py`)**: `strip_analysis_state_secrets` now blanks endpoint fields (`llm_base_url`, `base_url`, `deep_research_llm_base_url`, `proxy_url`) alongside the credentials, and both LLM client factories fall back to the global client when an agent has a custom endpoint but no key of its own. Export stripped `api_key` and kept `llm_base_url`, so an imported session built a client pairing the archive's endpoint with the destination's key — every call failed, and a hand-edited archive could name any host.
  - **`config_io` writes the endpoint key the runtime reads (`backend/services/config_io.py`)**: `build_agent_server_configs` emits `llm_base_url`, not `base_url`. Both consumers read `llm_base_url`, so an imported configuration silently lost its endpoint while keeping the credential that belonged to it, sending that credential to the destination's global provider.
  - **Path remapping is anchored and blob-aware (`backend/routes/export_import_parts/import_impl.py`, `backend/config/_config.py`)**: `_remap_paths_in_text` replaces the source data root only at a delimiter followed by `/`, refuses a relative root rather than guessing, and now also runs over the blob-inlined state. `DATA_BASE_DIR` is resolved to an absolute path. An unanchored `str.replace` of the Docker image's `/data` rewrote every `https://host/data/...` URL and `/dataset/...` literal in the document, and strings ≥ 1 KB (code history, report segments) live in byte-copied `blobs/*.b64` sidecars that the first remap never saw.
  - **Archive member filtering is segment-wise (`backend/routes/export_import_parts/import_impl.py`)**: new `_is_unsafe_member_name` rejects a `..` path *segment* instead of the substring. The old test silently dropped ordinary analysis output such as `DEG_A..B.csv`, so the restored session was missing files the archive contained and the job still reported success.
  - **Nothing is written before the state validates (`backend/routes/export_import_parts/import_impl.py`)**: `AnalysisState` is constructed before `session.json` is written, and `_process_import_job` now cleans up the session it created on every failure path — the previous cleanup was unreachable because its guard variable was assigned only after the extraction returned, while cancellation is raised inside it. A payload this build cannot load used to leave a directory that `list_saved_sessions` shows and `load_session_from_disk` refuses, producing a sidebar entry that could be neither opened nor deleted. Unknown manifest versions are warned about, and a manifest whose `session_id` disagrees with the archive directory is rejected.
  - **Import jobs are scoped to their owner, and extraction stops on a full disk (`backend/routes/export_import_parts/import_routes.py`, `backend/routes/export_import_parts/core.py`)**: job records carry `user_id`; status and cancel report 404 for anyone else, and a client-chosen `X-Import-Id` cannot take over a live job (409). `MIN_FREE_DISK_BYTES` / `free_disk_bytes` abort extraction with 507 instead of filling the data volume.
  - **Prepared exports are unique and reclaimed (`backend/routes/export_import_parts/export_routes.py`, `backend/tasks/session_cleanup.py`, `backend/main.py`, `backend/config/_config.py`)**: `/export/prepare` writes to a unique temp path — the previous path was derived from the session id and name alone, so a second prepare truncated the archive the first was still writing — and new `_cleanup_stale_prepared_exports` deletes archives nobody downloaded after `PREPARED_EXPORT_TTL`.
  - **Export filename cap counts bytes (`backend/routes/export_import_parts/core.py`)** and **the upload header is percent-encoded (`frontend/src/components/Sidebar.tsx`, `backend/routes/export_import_parts/import_routes.py`)**: `slugify_session_name` keeps CJK, so a ~78-character Chinese session name exceeded the filesystem's 255-*byte* limit and made that session permanently un-exportable, while `xhr.setRequestHeader('X-Filename', file.name)` threw outright on any non-Latin-1 name — every session named in Chinese produced an archive its own UI could not re-upload.
  - **Both export endpoints share one builder (`backend/routes/export_import_parts/export_routes.py`)**: new `_build_session_tar` replaces two byte-identical copies that nonetheless stamped different manifest versions (`1.0` vs `2.0`); `ARCHIVE_FORMAT_VERSION` is now the single source. LLM interaction logs (`sessions/<id>/logs/`) are packed, so a migrated session's regenerated report no longer claims every agent made zero calls.
  - **Remaining import paths caught up (`backend/routes/export_import_parts/import_routes.py`, `import_impl.py`)**: the legacy zip path extracts uploads, reports and blob sidecars (not only the notebook and workspace) and applies the same remapping; the plain-JSON path inlines blobs and marks runtime availability; extension dispatch is case-insensitive and an unsupported format returns 400 instead of a bare 500 from `json.loads`.
  - **Chat delegation honours the runtime gate (`backend/routes/chat_parts/delegation.py`)**: bioinformatics delegation refuses when `is_forward_resume_blocked`, instead of starting an empty sandbox while its own prompt asserts every earlier result is loaded.
  - **Runtime warning is shown with Snapshot V2 off (`frontend/src/components/AnalysisPanel.tsx`)**: the banner was gated on `snapshotV2Enabled`, but an ordinary import sets `runtime_restore_status='unavailable'` with the subsystem disabled, leaving Resume disabled with the explanation suppressed.
  - **`ConfigV1` describes the sessions the product creates (`backend/models/config_schema.py`)**: `IndicatorEntry.unit` is optional (the PI agent writes `""` when a document states none) and the name/key length caps were dropped, since nothing bounds those values where they are set — the caps only made an existing session's configuration permanently un-exportable with a 500.
  - **New tests (`backend/tests/test_export_import_hardening.py`, `backend/tests/test_export_import_routes_hardening.py`)**: 42 cases covering import confinement, id collision and rebinding, stale-pkl runtime marking, segment-wise member filtering, anchored and blob-aware remapping, validate-before-write, link-free archives, log packing, byte-capped filenames, export authentication and token binding, prepared-export reclamation, job ownership, the disk-space floor, explicit-start re-run, the recompute prompt, endpoint/credential pairing, and the `config_io` key contract.

- `0.36.8` — Snapshot V2 restore/supersession signal scope, HITL action serialization, workflow-boundary Retry re-drive, continuation freeze gate detection, preflight resume-target validation, recovery concurrency guards, capture object re-verification, exporter partial-archive cleanup; version bump (2026-07-28):
  - **Superseded signal moved to process-wide SafePointManager (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/safe_point.py`, `backend/services/snapshot_v2/service.py`, `backend/workflow/core.py`)**: `_retire_superseded_workflow()` now calls `SafePointManager.mark_superseded(session_id)` instead of only setting `workflow._restore_superseded`. The restore evicts the workflow object, so a flag stored on it is unreachable to the bioinformatics step loop and every other site frozen on the safe point. All superseded-entry-point handlers now check `sp.is_superseded()` and call `clear_superseded()` after unwinding.
  - **Bioinformatics step loop superseded check (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: After the safe-point wait in the autonomous loop, the agent now checks `sp.is_superseded()` and raises `WorkflowSupersededError` instead of continuing on a closed checkpoint connection with a stale graph position.
  - **Superseded close skips kernel save (`backend/workflow/core.py`, `backend/utils/sandbox_parts/kernel.py`)**: `workflow.close()` checks the superseded signal and calls `sandbox.stop(save=False)`. `sandbox.stop()` gains a `save` parameter (default `True`). After a restore installed the authoritative pkl/notebook, saving would overwrite them with the pre-restore kernel.
  - **Resume pre-graph await wrapped in try/except (`backend/workflow/core.py`)**: `resume()`'s `await_restore_safe_point()` now catches `WorkflowSupersededError`. Previously this await sat outside every handler, so a restore committing while frozen there escaped into the caller's generic `except Exception` — which writes `TaskStatus.FAILED` over the state the restore just persisted.
  - **with_retry passes through WorkflowSupersededError (`backend/workflow/retry.py`)**: New `except WorkflowSupersededError: raise` clause prevents the generic handler from swallowing the signal and retrying a node against a deleted graph position on a closed connection.
  - **Headless runner catches WorkflowSupersededError (`backend/services/workflow_runner.py`)**: `_headless_workflow_runner` now catches `WorkflowSupersededError` and returns quietly instead of falling through to the generic handler that writes `FAILED`.
  - **Snapshot-failure HITL action serialization (`backend/state.py`, `backend/routes/lifecycle.py`)**: New `snapshot_action_locks` dict and `get_snapshot_action_lock()` serialize Retry/Skip/Terminate actions per session. Retry re-runs the failed capture while the gate stays armed; without serialization, an interleaved Terminate passes its `is_snapshot_failure_blocked()` check, closes the kernel the capture is reading, and has its `STOPPED` write overwritten by the finishing Retry. The lock is separate from `session_locks` because `SnapshotService.capture()` acquires that lock itself, and `asyncio.Lock` is not reentrant.
  - **Workflow-boundary Retry without live workflow arms graph re-drive (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/failure_gate.py`)**: `default_retry_workflow_boundary_capture` now calls `retry_workflow_boundary(state)` and returns success when no live workflow exists, instead of failing closed with `WORKFLOW_UNAVAILABLE`. The gate always tears the workflow down before the user can act, so failing closed made Retry impossible; the capture is re-attempted when the resumed graph re-hits the same boundary.
  - **Workflow-boundary Retry closes HITL WebSocket (`backend/routes/lifecycle.py`)**: After a successful workflow-boundary Retry, the handler closes the frontend's WebSocket with code 4000 (the same code the confirm/choose endpoints use), triggering the frontend's auto-reconnect which rebuilds the workflow for the re-drive.
  - **Stale skip marker cleared on new run and terminate (`backend/routes/lifecycle.py`, `backend/services/snapshot_v2/failure_gate.py`)**: `run_analysis` clears `skip_required_snapshot_phase` at the start of a fresh run. `terminate_snapshot_failure` also clears it. Previously, a Skip armed at a boundary the graph never reached again stayed on the session indefinitely; the wildcard form (`*`, armed when the failing phase is unknown) suppressed whichever capture happened to come first in a later, unrelated run.
  - **Parked session restore acknowledgement (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/jobs.py`)**: `_acknowledge_if_quiescent` now allows a session parked at a HITL gate (status starts with `waiting`) to acknowledge a restore even when a workflow object exists. The object outlives its run; the cooperative handshake needs a running graph to reach a boundary, and a parked session never will. Without this, a restore requested at a gate had nowhere to be acknowledged and could only time out on a completely idle session.
  - **Continuation dynamic freeze reads session status (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/continuation.py`)**: `freeze_dynamic` now inspects `analysis_state.status` to detect which HITL gate the session is parked at, and produces a `semantic_gate` capsule instead of always falling through to `Idle`. Previously, every dynamic capture of a running session (including `pre_restore` — the one snapshot a user reaches for to undo a restore) recorded no position at all, because the descriptor-driven branches read keys no producer ever writes.
  - **Preflight validates resume target before apply (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/preflight.py`)**: `RestorePreflight.run()` now checks that the capsule's graph node or gate exists in the current build's `_KNOWN_GRAPH_NODES` / `_KNOWN_GATES`. Previously these checks only ran in `stage_checkpoint`, after `apply()` had already replaced the live session — an imported snapshot from another instance or a pre-upgrade snapshot passed preflight, destroyed the session, and was then rejected as unknown, leaving it recovery-blocked.
  - **Pre-restore capture records round_number (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/pre_restore.py`)**: `PreRestoreCapture` now sets `round_number` on the capture request from the live state's `current_round`. `live_verify` compares `manifest.round_number` against the restored session's `current_round` and gates on `is not None`, so the model default (0) was asserted as a real expectation. Left defaulted, every pre_restore snapshot taken past round 0 was unrestorable: apply overwrites the live session, the round check fails, and the transaction is refused as `LIVE_VERIFY_FAILED`.
  - **Dispatcher activate carries skip_nodes to sandbox_state (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/dispatcher.py`)**: `activate()` now copies `staged.payload["skip_nodes"]` into `sandbox_state["skip_nodes"]`, which `resume()` reads and injects into the graph state for `@with_retry`. Previously, capture froze skip_nodes but activate only stashed it under `restore_gate_payload` — a key nothing reads — so every restore silently discarded Skip decisions and re-entered the node the user had skipped past.
  - **Dispatcher sets gate-correct status (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/dispatcher.py`)**: `activate()` now sets `TaskStatus.WAITING_ANNOTATION` for `annotation_confirmation` gates and `WAITING_INPUT` for all others. Previously every gate was set to `WAITING_INPUT`, so a restored session parked at the annotation gate could not be acted on (the `/confirm-annotations` endpoint rejects anything but `WAITING_ANNOTATION`).
  - **ensure_post_report_retry_context producer guard (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/failure_gate.py`)**: The helper now returns early when the existing gate's `retry_context.producer` is not `post_report`. Previously it rewrote `allowed_actions` and `retry_context` unconditionally, converting a `pre_restore` or `workflow_boundary` gate into a `post_report` one — which added an invalid Skip and dispatched Retry to the wrong producer.
  - **Restore job finally releases safe point (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/jobs.py`)**: `_run_job` now releases the safe point in a `finally` block using `asyncio.shield()`. Previously, a journal-write failure in an `except` handler (ENOSPC, read-only mount) skipped the release and stranded the hold in the process-wide `SafePointManager` — every later capture and restore for the session failed `SAFE_POINT_BUSY` until restart. Same structural gap that was closed in `SnapshotService._capture_impl` in 0.36.7.
  - **Restore job always persists terminal handle (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/jobs.py`)**: `_run_job` now writes the journal record for every terminal outcome, including `recovery_required` failures. Previously those were skipped, leaving the journal at `waiting_safe_point` forever — nothing ever reconciles it, so the snapshot was undeletable and status polls after a restart read `waiting_safe_point` indefinitely.
  - **Journal owner identity for live-import detection (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/journal_store.py`, `backend/services/snapshot_v2/importer.py`, `backend/services/snapshot_v2/recovery.py`)**: New `JOURNAL_OWNER_ID` (per-process `uuid.uuid4().hex`) stamped into import journal payloads. Recovery's `_recover_import_envelope` now defers journals whose owner matches the current process, instead of rolling back a live import. An import runs in a worker thread while the event loop keeps serving requests; every request runs recovery, which previously dropped the STAGING journal and let post-recovery GC sweep objects the import was still streaming.
  - **Recovery defers while session lock held (`backend/services/session_store.py`)**: `ensure_session_loaded` now skips recovery when the per-session lock is held (`_snapshot_transaction_in_flight`). Journal recovery is crash recovery, but it ran on every authenticated request — including the frontend's 2-second poll of the restore's own status endpoint — while the restore holds the lock and yields. A request served at one of those yields ran the entire rollback and destroyed the restore it was asked to report on. Deferred recovery also does not clear an existing recovery block (distinguishing "ran and found nothing" from "was deferred").
  - **Recovery defers GC while safe point held (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/recovery.py`)**: Post-recovery GC now checks `_gc_blocked_by_active_safe_point()` before sweeping. A capture writes no journal, so `mark()` has nothing protecting the objects it has already put; sweeping them lets the capture publish a committed manifest whose refs point at deleted files. A live import is also a reason to skip.
  - **Capture re-verifies object existence before publishing (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/service.py`)**: `_record_and_track` now checks that the object still exists in the store (via `_get_evidence` / `register_existing_ref`) after the last suspension point. Objects put before the runtime capture's awaits have no journal protection; concurrent GC can remove them. The capture now fails closed instead of publishing a manifest with dangling refs.
  - **live_verify checks analysis_state object in store (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/live_verify.py`)**: When `objects_root` is provided, `verify_live_restore_components` now checks that the analysis_state object file actually exists. Previously it only asserted `ref.sha256` was truthy — a non-optional, pattern-validated field that is always a 64-hex string, so the check could never fail and never touched the store.
  - **Exporter removes partial archive on failure (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/exporter.py`)**: `SnapshotExporter.export` now deletes the temp archive in a `finally` block when the export did not complete. Any failure after `mkstemp` (missing object, hash mismatch, ENOSPC) previously left a multi-GB partial file in the system temp dir; only the success path moved it somewhere owned.
  - **New tests (`backend/tests/test_snapshot_v2_runtime_edges.py`, `backend/tests/test_snapshot_v2_restore_jobs.py`)**: 17 new test cases covering superseded signal scope, bioinformatics step loop honouring, superseded close skip-save, preflight unknown-target rejection, post_report producer guard, stale skip-marker clearing, parked-session acknowledgement, continuation gate detection, live_verify missing-object detection, recovery deferral under lock and safe point, exporter partial-archive cleanup, with_retry pass-through, restore-job finally release, resume pre-graph try/except, and dispatcher skip_nodes/status fixes.

- `0.36.7` — Snapshot V2 restore correctness: WAL checkpoint shadowing, rollback h5ad manifest rewrite, rollback checkpoint restoration, workflow supersession; GC and deletion orphan-marker fixes; AnnData uns integer-key sanitization; runtime-registry AnnData type matching; safe-point leaked-hold fix; version bump (2026-07-28):
  - **Pre-restore workflow retirement before apply (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/service.py`)**: New `_retire_superseded_workflow()` runs *before* `apply()` instead of after. It (1) flags the run superseded (`_restore_superseded=True`), so the frozen workflow unwinds via `WorkflowSupersededError` instead of resuming with a stale graph position; (2) closes the checkpointer's SQLite connection, which is required because the DB is in WAL mode — an open connection keeps live `-wal`/`-shm` files that survive `apply()`'s replacement of the main file and are replayed over it afterwards, silently destroying the restored checkpoint; (3) evicts the workflow from the global `workflows` dict so the next run rebuilds against the restored file. Terminal/idle kernel teardown also moved before `apply()` so `save_session()` does not overwrite the runtime artifacts `apply()` is about to install.
  - **WorkflowSupersededError (`backend/workflow/helpers.py`, `backend/workflow/core.py`)**: New exception raised when a restore commits while the workflow is frozen at a safe-point. Unlike `WorkflowTerminatedError`, it deliberately does NOT touch session state — the restore already persisted the authoritative state, and overwriting it with a terminal status would corrupt the restore. Caught at all three run-loop entry points (run, resume, restore-continue) to return the restored state untouched.
  - **Auto-snapshot yields to pending restore (`backend/workflow/core.py`)**: `_create_auto_snapshot` now checks for a pending restore safe-point request before acquiring its own. Without this, a pending restore request blocked the capture's acquire (`SAFE_POINT_BUSY`), which aborted the graph invocation — so the workflow never reached another acknowledgement site, the restore timed out, and the user's restore request destroyed the running analysis and then failed.
  - **Safe-point leaked-hold fix (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/service.py`)**: `capture()` now releases the safe point in a `finally` block using `asyncio.shield()`. Previously, `asyncio.CancelledError` (from user Stop or workflow teardown mid-capture) bypassed both `except` clauses (it derives from `BaseException`), leaving the hold in the process-wide `SafePointManager` forever — every subsequent capture and restore for that session failed `SAFE_POINT_BUSY` until server restart.
  - **Rollback h5ad manifest rewrite (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/recovery.py`, `backend/utils/runtime_apply.py`)**: Rollback now rewrites the live h5ad sidecar manifest to match the pre-restore files it just put back, and quarantines sidecars the target installed that pre-restore does not name. Previously, rollback copied the h5ad files but left the manifest describing the *target* snapshot; the kernel checks h5ad hashes against the manifest on restore and refuses to rehydrate on mismatch — so the rollback meant to rescue the session was what stopped its kernel from starting. New `finalize_live_runtime_h5ad_set()` extracted from `install_live_runtime_h5ad_set()` for callers that already placed the bytes themselves.
  - **Rollback checkpoint restoration (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/recovery.py`)**: Rollback now restores the pre-restore LangGraph checkpoint DB. `apply()` replaces it with the target's copy, so a rollback that skips it leaves the session resuming from the TARGET position against a pre-restore workspace and runtime. Pre-restore's WAL frames are deliberately preserved (they contain graph progress the snapshot could not capture). New `_discard_sqlite_db()` helper removes a WAL-mode database together with its `-wal`/`-shm` companions — used when pre-restore had no checkpoint (the whole triple must go).
  - **GC orphan pending-import marker tolerance (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/gc.py`)**: An import marks every planned id pending before publishing any manifest, so a crash in between leaves a marker with no manifest. Loading it raised, which aborted the whole sweep — and since GC is the only reclaimer, one orphan marker permanently bricked both GC and deletion. Now skipped with a warning; the import-rollback path removes the marker itself.
  - **Deletion pending-manifest marker guard (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/deletion.py`)**: `_delete_pending_imports` now requires the pending-import marker to exist before deleting (a retried or stale rollback can name an id that has since been published by a successful retry or unrelated capture — without this check that snapshot is silently destroyed). The marker is removed unconditionally and last, so an import that died between `mark_pending_imports()` and publish (leaving a marker whose manifest never existed) no longer survives every rollback.
  - **AnnData uns integer-key sanitization (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/runtime_parts/anndata_sanitize.py`, `backend/services/snapshot_v2/runtime_parts/kernel_templates.py`)**: Both the host-side `sanitize_anndata_for_h5ad()` and the kernel-side `_SAVE_ANNDATA_TEMPLATE` now coerce non-string `uns` keys to `str` before h5ad write. Cluster-id → label maps (e.g. `uns['cluster_labels'] = {0: 'T cell'}`) with integer or numpy-integer keys previously failed inside h5py with `'int' object has no attribute 'split'`, taking the whole required capture down. Key collisions (renaming would clobber an existing entry) are dropped instead of silently overwriting.
  - **Runtime-registry AnnData type matching fix (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/runtime_registry.py`)**: `_is_anndata_type()` now matches only `AnnData` itself (shapes `"AnnData(<shape>)"` from the kernel introspection template, or `"<module>.AnnData"` from a qualname). Previously it substring-matched `"anndata"`, which also matched internal helper types (`anndata._core.raw.Raw`, `anndata._core.aligned_mapping.AxisArrays`, etc.) that have no `.write()` method — marking them as required with `serializer="h5ad"` made the h5ad template raise `AttributeError` and parked the session behind the snapshot HITL gate where Retry reproduced it forever.

- `0.36.6` — Cross-instance import resumability, sandbox save full-namespace with best-effort tier, credential decryption graceful degradation; version bump (2026-07-28):
  - **Import runtime availability marking (`backend/routes/export_import_parts/import_impl.py`)**: New `_mark_runtime_availability_after_import()` sets `runtime_restore_status="unavailable"` when an imported session has `current_round > 0` but no live sandbox pkl — the workflow's `is_forward_resume_blocked` gate then tells the user to re-run from imported data instead of resuming into an empty kernel. Completed sessions are unaffected (the gate exempts them). Applied to both tar and zip import paths.
  - **Import pkl re-signing (`backend/routes/export_import_parts/import_impl.py`)**: New `_resign_imported_live_pkl()` regenerates the HMAC sidecar under the destination installation's key after importing a sandbox pkl with `trust=true`. The sidecar lives outside the session directory and is never in a portable archive; without a local signature `verify_live_pkl_or_raise` refuses rehydration and sandbox startup fails. Mirrors Snapshot V2 restore's `apply_runtime_pkl` convention.
  - **Export checkpoint co-travel with pkl (`backend/routes/export_import_parts/tar_builders.py`)**: `_add_states_to_tar` now includes the LangGraph checkpoint alongside the sandbox pkl when `include_runtime=true`, since the two are only useful together (checkpoint says which node to resume at; pkl holds the kernel values that node expects). Previous behavior withheld the checkpoint because it carried sealed credentials; `open_secret` now degrades un-decryptable values to empty instead of failing the load, so cross-instance credential mismatch no longer blocks checkpoint deserialization.
  - **Sandbox save full-namespace with best-effort tier (`backend/utils/sandbox.py`)**: `save_session` now saves the *entire* user namespace instead of only tracker-declared and canonical names. Two-tier model: **Required** (tracker-declared, canonical artifacts that exist, and every AnnData regardless of name) — fail-closed, missing/unpersistable aborts the save. **Everything else** — best-effort, unpicklable values (open loom/h5py handles, sockets, locks) are skipped and reported via `_last_save_skipped` instead of failing the save. Modules, functions, classes and methods are excluded (re-established by kernel init + notebook replay; by-reference frames are the most common restore-breaker across environments). Streaming writes only for required values and numeric containers (numpy/pandas/scipy/anndata); optional values are serialized in full before writing so a mid-write failure does not desync the stream. Previously, a stray unpicklable file handle in the namespace would abort the entire save.
  - **Credential decryption graceful degradation (`backend/utils/secret_redaction.py`)**: `open_secret()` now catches decryption failures and returns an empty string instead of raising. A secret sealed under a different installation key (normal for cross-instance imports or `SCAI_CONFIG_SECRET` rotation) no longer aborts `AnalysisState` validation, which would make the LangGraph checkpoint impossible to deserialize and block workflow resume. The credential is operational configuration, not scientific state — the user reconfigures it.
  - **Admin manual export/import documentation rewrite (`docs/admin-manual.md`, `docs/admin-manual-zh.md`)**: Replaced outdated "does not preserve active in-memory execution" with detailed explanation of the `include_runtime=true/false` choice, cross-instance resumability, pkl re-signing, credential handling, and the requirement that the session was exported while paused/waiting-for-input. Updated both English and Chinese versions.
  - **Sidebar export/import dialog text update (`frontend/src/components/Sidebar.tsx`)**: Both the runtime-inclusion and trust-confirmation confirm dialogs updated to reflect that runtime exports now resume on any instance (not just the original), and that declining runtime/pkl means the imported session cannot continue and must be re-run from data.

- `0.36.5` — Painter agent protocol timeout increase, version bump (2026-07-28):
  - **Painter protocol timeout increase (`backend/agents/painter_agent.py`)**: Per-protocol attempt timeout raised from 60s to 180s; revision total timeout raised from 75s to 720s. Accommodates slower image-generation endpoints (especially deep-research and multi-provider cascades) that routinely exceed the previous tight budgets, preventing premature aborts on valid in-progress requests.
  - **HANDOVER version history back-fill**: Restored condensed version-history entries for 0.1.x–0.34.x that were truncated in prior edits; reordered 0.34.9 evidence/credential entries to their correct version position; added missing `0.32.11.1`, `0.31.5.fix`, `0.25.16` entries.

- `0.36.4` — Documentation consolidation and version bump (2026-07-27):
  - Added bilingual biomedical user guides with screenshots and migrated bilingual administrator manuals into `docs/`.
  - Rewrote the README as a concise project entry point and moved/updated the technical handover under `docs/`.
  - Removed public descriptions of unreleased functionality; corrected deployment, Python client, and session-management documentation.

- `0.36.3` — New-session form default-value visual hint, form reset on creation, default instruction rewrite; version bump (2026-07-25):
  - **Default-value visual hint in NewSessionForm (`frontend/src/components/sidebar/NewSessionForm.tsx`)**: Session name and instruction fields now render their default values in placeholder-style gray (`text-surface-400`) when the content is still the unmodified default, switching to normal text (`text-surface-800`) once the user edits or focuses the field. Tracks `isDefaultName`/`isDefaultInstruction` and `isFocusedName`/`isFocusedInstruction` states so the gray hint disappears on focus and reappears on blur if the value is still the default. Imports `DEFAULT_SESSION_NAME` and `DEFAULT_SESSION_INSTRUCTION` from `sessionDefaults.ts` for comparison.
  - **Form reset on session creation (`frontend/src/components/Sidebar.tsx`)**: New `formKey` state incremented after successful session creation, passed as `key` to `NewSessionForm` to force a full remount — clearing the local default-tracking state that would otherwise persist stale `isDefault*` flags into the next creation.
  - **Default instruction rewrite (`frontend/src/constants/sessionDefaults.ts`)**: Default `DEFAULT_SESSION_INSTRUCTION` expanded from a single paragraph to a structured Background + Objective format. Background section provides research context; Objective section states the central analysis goal in domain-neutral terms. `DEFAULT_SESSION_NAME` unchanged.

- `0.36.2` — Split-facade test helper rename (`defines` → `binds`), emptied-facade module-read guard, frontend split doc/comment cleanup, upload types deduplication; version bump (2026-07-25):
  - **`split_facade.defines()` renamed to `binds()` (`backend/tests/split_facade.py`)**: The old name implied "defines" (i.e. implements), but the function reports every namespace that *binds* a name — including re-exports. Renamed to `binds()` to match semantics; `holders()` now delegates to `binds()`. Docstrings expanded to explain the distinction: `binds` is for absence assertions (a re-export is just as reachable), `holders` is for patching (must not patch nothing).
  - **Emptied-facade module-read guard (`backend/tests/test_facade_patch_targets.py`)**: New `test_no_test_reads_an_emptied_facade_through_its_module` AST-scans the test suite for `open(mod.__file__)` / `inspect.getsource(mod)` reads targeting a facade whose implementation has been fully moved out (no `def`/`class` left). Reports only emptied facades — partial splits still hold implementation text, so reading them is correct. New `_is_emptied_facade()` and `_module_source_reads()` helpers. The existing `test_no_test_reads_a_split_facade_as_raw_source` now reports literal-path reads for *every* registered facade (not just non-thin ones), since `source_for_path` is a drop-in replacement.
  - **Test callers updated (`backend/tests/test_pi_agent_name_constant.py`, `backend/tests/test_schema_imports.py`)**: Both switched from `defines` to `binds`; assertion messages updated ("still define" → "still bind").
  - **Frontend split doc/comment cleanup**: `DataPanel.tsx` comment fixed ("API layer" → "data panel"), added export-rationale note for module-private pieces. `DataOverviewMatrix.tsx` split comment updated to mention the Map-based count rewrite. `uploadRules.ts` `SEQUENCING_TYPES` now derives from `utils/dataTypes` instead of restating the list (the two had already drifted). Stale section-divider comments removed from `client.ts`, `useConfig.ts`, `useSession.ts`.
  - **No intentional behavior change** beyond the upload-types deduplication.

- `0.36.1` — Merge the `ver-0.35.1-rc` facade-split branch into `main`; restore the structural guards the split had silently disarmed; version bump (2026-07-25):
  - **Facade split landed on `main`**: ~20 monolithic modules are now a thin re-export facade plus a sibling `*_parts/` (or mixin) package — `pi_agent` (5061 → 299 lines), `bioinformatics_agent` (6256 → 2963), `sandbox` (2359 → 508), `llm_client`, `supervisor_agent`, `literature_agent`, `schemas` → `schema_parts`, `workflow/agents` → `agent_nodes`, `workflow/reporting`, `routes/{chat,export_import,reports}`, `snapshot_v2/{restore,runtime,service}`, `report/variants`; plus the frontend splits of `appStore.ts`, `useApi.ts`, `translations.ts`, `DataPanel.tsx`, `Sidebar.tsx`, `AnalysisPanel.tsx`. Behavior-preserving: routes (156), Pydantic models (149), composite-class member sets, the LangGraph node/edge registry, and all 373 × 2 i18n keys are unchanged.
  - **Merge resolution**: `main`'s 0.35.2/0.35.3 edits to the pre-split `reporting.py` are carried by `reporting_parts/final_report.py` (character-identical logic), so the facade form wins the conflict; `test_autonomous_partial_report.py` keeps the `setattr_facade` form, since patching the facade alone no longer reaches `_generate_final_report`.
  - **Structural guards repaired (`backend/tests/`)**: a class of tests asserts on raw module source text or on `hasattr()`. After the split they still inspected the facade — now only a re-export header — so their *negative* assertions passed unconditionally. Seven source-reading sites now go through `split_source()` (`test_pi_stop_keywords`, `test_bioinformatics_dependency_policy`, `test_tpl_noop_install` ×3, `test_durable_file_primitives`, `test_snapshot_v2_pre_restore`), and two absence checks now walk the whole split via the new `split_facade.defines()` (`test_pi_agent_name_constant`, `test_schema_imports`). Each was verified by injecting a violation into a *part* module and confirming the guard fails.
  - **One genuinely dead guard fixed (`backend/tests/test_p3_routes_housekeeping.py`)**: the export-filename cap check read `export_import.py`, whose only match was the name `MAX_EXPORT_NAME` inside the re-export list — the cap itself lives in `export_import_parts/core.py`. It now reads the split.
  - **Recurrence guard added (`backend/tests/test_facade_patch_targets.py`)**: `test_no_test_reads_a_split_facade_as_raw_source` AST-scans the suite for raw facade source reads, resolving one level of local path binding and stripping `__import__()` module names so all five historical read shapes are caught. Scope is documented: `open(module.__file__)` / `inspect.getsource(module)` are not flagged (of 15 such sites aimed at a registered facade, 14 assert on text the facade still holds).
  - **Facade duplicate constants removed**: eight module-level constants were defined in a facade *and* in its parts — `_ORA_KERNEL_SRC`, `MAX_WORKSPACE_H5AD_FILES` (`bioinformatics_agent.py`), `_SPECIFIC_SECTION_IDS`, `_SECTION_ID_TO_DEFAULT_TITLE`, `_PLACEHOLDER_SECTION_TITLES` (`supervisor_agent.py`), `IOPUB_POLL_CAP` (`sandbox.py`), `REPORT_ARCHIVE_PREFIX` (`reports.py`), `_SPECIES_TISSUE_LABELS` (`variants.py`). Six were shadowed by a later re-export; the last two were live divergent copies with no re-export at all. All now have exactly one definition, in the part.
  - **`split_facade.py`**: `split_modules()` made public; new non-raising `defines(facade, attr)` with `holders()` redefined on top of it (`holders` raises, `defines` does not — absence assertions need the latter).
  - **No intentional behavior change**: backend suite unchanged at 8 pre-existing failures / 3200 passed; frontend `tsc`, `vitest` (208), and `vite build` all clean.

**0.35.x — (2026-07-24)**

- `0.35.4` — Merge `main` (0.35.3) into the facade-split refactor branch; version bump (2026-07-24):
  - Brings the auto-advancing partial-report gate and the expression-contract local-copy shielding + docstring clarifications (from 0.35.2 and 0.35.3) onto the facade-split (`*_parts/`) codebase.
  - **Report gate auto-advance re-applied in the split layout (`backend/workflow/reporting_parts/final_report.py`)**: `main` added the partial-report auto-advance to the pre-split monolithic `reporting.py`; here it is ported onto `FinalReportMixin._generate_final_report`, while the top-level `reporting.py` stays the thin re-export facade.
  - **No intentional behavior change** beyond the union of both branches; version string updated to `0.35.4`.

- `0.35.3` — Report gate auto-advance for partial reports; expression-contract docstrings; version bump (2026-07-24):
  - **Report gate auto-advance in auto-advancing modes (`backend/workflow/reporting.py`)**: When direction coverage is incomplete and `round_wait_seconds >= 0` (no-wait or countdown mode), the final-report gate now produces a partial report instead of blocking indefinitely. Manual mode (`round_wait_seconds == -1`) retains the block-and-wait behavior. New `allow_partial_report` flag on `AnalysisState`.
  - **Expression-contract docstring clarifications (`backend/utils/expression_contract.py`)**: Added docstrings explaining the conservative closed-set design of `_FRESH_OBJECT_CALLS` (safe false positives over stale-figure false negatives) and the intentional asymmetry of `prepares_expression_before_sensitive_call` (heuristic, not figure-invalidation).
  - **New tests (`backend/tests/test_autonomous_partial_report.py`)**: `test_auto_advancing_mode_produces_partial_instead_of_blocking` (parametrized for wait=0 and wait=300), `test_manual_mode_still_blocks_and_waits`.

- `0.35.2` — Expression contract local-copy shielding; version bump (2026-07-24):
  - **Local-copy mutation is not a representation change (`backend/utils/expression_contract.py`)**: `mutates_expression_representation()` now tracks local-object assignments via AST event replay. Mutations of throwaway copies (e.g. `work = adata.copy(); sc.pp.log1p(work)`) no longer invalidate prior figures. Fresh-object calls (`copy`, `read_h5ad`, `AnnData`, `concat`) and aliases of already-local names are shielded. Canonical-object mutations alongside local copies are still correctly detected. New `_FRESH_OBJECT_CALLS` set, `_mutation_target_root()`, `_value_makes_fresh_object()`, and event-sorted replay loop.
  - **New tests (`backend/tests/test_expression_contract.py`)**: `test_local_copy_mutation_is_not_a_representation_change` (6 sub-cases: copy, semicolon, existence-guarded, subset, fresh-read, local-X-replace), `test_canonical_mutation_still_detected_alongside_local_copies` (2 sub-cases: mixed copy+canonical, rebind to non-fresh source).

- `0.35.1-rc-0.3` — Facade split cleanup: remove duplicated constants after module split; DataOverviewMatrix count pre-index; version-test regex fix; version bump (2026-07-24):
  - **Bioinformatics shared constants (`backend/agents/bioinformatics/`)**: Dropped accidental duplicate imports (`inspect`/`ora_kernel`) and per-module `MAX_WORKSPACE_H5AD_FILES` copies from `kernel_templates.py`, `mechanism_validation.py`, `tool_schemas.py`, and `workspace_guards.py`. Workspace guard now imports `MAX_WORKSPACE_H5AD_FILES` from `shared.py`.
  - **Schema parts cleanup (`backend/models/schema_parts/`)**: Removed duplicated module-level constants (`DEFAULT_ROUND_WAIT_SECONDS`, sequencing data-type strings, `_version_lock`) from `analysis.py`, `enums.py`, `outputs.py`, and `session.py` — canonical definitions remain on the parent facade (`schemas.py`).
  - **Report routes cleanup (`backend/routes/reports_parts/`)**: Removed per-part `REPORT_ARCHIVE_PREFIX` duplicates from `assets.py`, `html_views.py`, and `publication.py`; `downloads.py` imports the shared constant from `core.py`.
  - **Report variants cleanup (`backend/services/report/variants_parts/`)**: Removed duplicated `_SPECIES_TISSUE_LABELS` from `annotation.py`, `detailed.py`, `exploration.py`, and `integration.py` (canonical map lives in `renderers.py`).
  - **Publication figures endpoint (`backend/routes/reports_parts/publication.py`)**: Single-pass build of figure list + approved count (no second scan over `state.publication_figures`).
  - **Data overview matrix (`frontend/src/components/data/DataOverviewMatrix.tsx`)**: Pre-index file/indicator counts by `groupId` + section with `Map`s instead of repeated `filter` scans per group/section cell.

- `0.35.1-rc-0.2` — Continue facade split: chat, export/import, LLM client, workflow agent/reporting nodes; version bump (2026-07-23):

- `0.35.1-rc-0.1` — Large-module facade split refactor (behavior-preserving); version bump (2026-07-23):
  - **Backend agent packages**: Split monolithic agent modules into packages with stable facades — `bioinformatics/` (loop control, planning, figures, mechanism validation, workspace guards, kernel templates), `literature/` (PubMed, clients, query builders, synthesis, direction novelty), `pi/` (synthesis, instructions, research planning, direction candidates/selection, annotation, species, reports, figures, bioinfo liaison), `supervisor/` (evaluation, runtime, report HTML/content, diagrams). Original `*_agent.py` modules remain import-compatible re-export facades.
  - **Schema / routes / services / sandbox split**: `models/schema_parts/` (enums, analysis, session, outputs); `routes/reports_parts/` (core, html views, downloads, publication, assets); `services/report/variants_parts/` (exploration, detailed, integration, annotation, renderers); `services/snapshot_v2/{restore,runtime,service}_parts/`; `utils/sandbox_parts/` (kernel, execution, persistence, state tracking, paths). Parent modules re-export public symbols for existing importers and tests.
  - **Frontend modularization**: `DataPanel` / `Sidebar` / `AnalysisPanel` split into `components/data/*`, `components/sidebar/*`, and `components/analysis/RoundSummaries`; `useApi` into `hooks/api/*` (client, useSession, useConfig, useWebSocket, snapshots); Zustand store into `stores/{domain,appState,persistence,store}` with `appStore.ts` as the stable public surface; i18n into `locales/en.ts` + `locales/zh.ts` + `prompts.ts` with `translations.ts` re-exports.
  - **Test adapter support**: `backend/tests/split_facade.py` and `test_facade_patch_targets.py` keep monkeypatch targets aligned with the new module layout.
  - **No intentional behavior change** — structural refactor only; version string updated to `0.35.1-rc-0.1` (previously labeled `0.35.1-rc1` in docs).

- `0.35.1` — Domain-neutral mechanism evidence policy, planning-failure terminal routing, plan schema normalization, domain-example scrub; version bump (2026-07-22):
  - **Shared mechanism evidence policy (`backend/utils/mechanism_policy.py`)**: New `MECHANISM_EVIDENCE_POLICY` constant — start with unbiased analyses; name established mechanisms only when observed evidence supports them; treat objective-named mechanisms as prespecified hypotheses (supported / contradicted / insufficient); distinguish pathway activity/priming from causal mechanism execution. Wired into PI direction/synthesis/report prompts, bioinformatics `register_mechanism_programs` tool + Stage-2 capture instruction, Supervisor live report prompt, and detailed-report planning fallback.
  - **Exploration preserves prespecified hypotheses without biasing baseline (`backend/agents/pi_agent.py`)**: `initial_exploration_instruction` still forbids objective-driven narrowing of mandatory broad tasks, but now requires an explicit post-baseline evidence assessment for mechanisms named in the research objective (support / contradiction / insufficient evidence) instead of silent omission or default confirmation.
  - **Mechanism program validation no longer injects a generic alternative (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: Removed `_DEFAULT_ALTERNATIVE` auto-fill; empty `alternative_explanations` stay empty. Default missing-evidence text retained when the field is absent.
  - **Plan schema normalization + bounded repair (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: New `_prepare_plan_data()` maps label-style step IDs (e.g. `"S2_Step_0"`) to deterministic positive integers, rewrites `depends_on`, reserves completed-step IDs during revision, and converts schema failures into `parse_error` so `_retry_on_parse_failure` can request one repair instead of leaking Pydantic errors into routing. `revise_plan_incrementally` freezes completed step title/method/rationale (only `depends_on` may change).
  - **Planning failure is terminal FAILED, not a successful report (`backend/workflow/planning.py`, `backend/workflow/routing.py`, `backend/workflow/graph.py`)**: Planning dialogue / review aborts set `TaskStatus.FAILED`, emit `planning_aborted` state_sync, and set `failed_node="planning_dialogue"`. New `_after_planning_dialogue` routes `"failed" → END` instead of `user_review_gate`; `_after_review` also ends on planning failure so a missing plan never becomes a completed final report.
  - **Resume preserves FAILED at graph end (`backend/workflow/core.py`)**: When the graph has no remaining nodes, resume no longer upgrades `FAILED`/`STOPPED` to `COMPLETED`; only non-terminal statuses are marked complete.
  - **Domain-neutral example scrub across agents and defaults**: Removed hard-coded tissue/disease/pathway examples from tool schemas, prompts, defaults, and i18n (pathology feature checklist, biochemistry indicator panel / experiment suggestions, literature contamination plausibility tissue table, PI annotation/MeSH/data-need examples, diagram node examples, frontend default pathology/biochemistry prompts). `plausibility_terms_for_tissue()` is now a no-op compatibility hook returning `[]`.
  - **New tests**: `test_mechanism_evidence_policy.py` (shared policy wiring + neutrality), `test_workflow_resume_terminal_status.py` (FAILED preserved on resume). Extended: plan-parse retry, mechanism tool schema/validation, workflow routing, planning dialogue failure, focused-query builder, pathology tile/grading, stage-2 mechanism instruction, rare-celltype batch replay.

**0.33.x–0.34.x — (2026-07-15)**

- `0.34.9` — Credential encryption at rest, execution evidence provenance, dense-memory resource preflight, cluster cell counts, integration phase persistence, sandbox safety bypass; version bump (2026-07-21):
  - **Credential encryption at rest (`backend/utils/secret_redaction.py`, `backend/models/schemas.py`, `backend/main.py`, `backend/routes/config.py`, `backend/routes/user_config.py`, `backend/routes/session_config.py`, `backend/routes/export_import.py`, `backend/services/snapshot_v2/service.py`)**: New `secret_redaction` module with `seal_analysis_state_payload()` / `open_analysis_state_payload()` for transparent AES-GCM encryption of `custom_models`, `agent_server_configs`, and `custom_prompts` in serialized `AnalysisState`. `seal_system_config_payload()` / `open_system_config_payload()` for global `api_key`/`proxy_url`. `mask_api_key()` shared masking utility. `strip_analysis_state_secrets()` for export/snapshot sanitization. `preserve_runtime_secrets()` for Snapshot V2 restore. `AnalysisState` uses Pydantic `model_serializer`/`model_validator` for automatic encrypt/decrypt on `model_dump`/`model_validate`. Global config migration on startup upgrades plaintext credentials to encrypted storage. User config route preserves masked preview values (no re-encryption of unchanged masked input). Agent server config route preserves masked preview. Export strips credentials from state JSON and excludes checkpoints from portable exports. Snapshot V2 capture strips credentials from serialized state.
  - **Log security hardening (`backend/config/_config.py`)**: New `_OwnerOnlyRotatingFileHandler` sets `0o600` on log files after creation. `SecretRedactionFilter` redacts API keys and proxy URLs from all log handlers (file + console). Log directory set to `0o700`. Existing log files remediated to `0o600`. `aiosqlite` logger set to WARNING to prevent SQL bind-parameter leakage (LangGraph checkpoints contain serialized `AnalysisState` with credentials).
  - **Execution evidence provenance (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`, `backend/models/schemas.py`, `backend/workflow/synthesis.py`)**: New `evidence_execution_ids` field on `report_findings` and `register_mechanism_programs` tools — findings must cite at least one successful current-run `execute_code` ID. `execution_supports_evidence()` filters out failed/contradictory executions. `successful_evidence_executions()` scopes to current round. `validate_mechanism_program_payload()` requires `evidence_execution_ids`. `derive_round_completion_status()` marks rounds with unverified evidence as `partial` with `direction_coverage_eligible=False`. New `RoundResult.bioinformatics_evidence_verified`, `evidence_execution_ids`, `evidence_notes` fields. PI and Supervisor agents receive evidence qualification instructions to never upgrade unverified claims. `CodeExecution` gains `round_number`, `evidence_eligible`, `evidence_rejection_reason`, and nullable `execution_time`. `AnalysisStep` gains `evidence_execution_ids`. `DataDrivenMechanismProgram` gains `evidence_execution_ids`. Code-history dedup changed from code-hash to execution-ID to preserve evidence provenance across retries.
  - **Dense-memory resource preflight (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: `_high_memory_referenced_names()` extended to detect `sc.pp.scale()` (unless `zero_center=False`) and `.X.toarray()`. `_build_resource_guard_preamble()` generates dense-matrix memory estimation (n_obs × n_vars × 8 bytes) with a hard cap at 1.5 GiB. `_HIGH_MEMORY_ANALYSIS_RE` made case-insensitive. Operation factor reduced to 2× for dense transforms vs 4–8× for graph operations.
  - **Cluster cell counts in annotation (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`, `backend/workflow/annotation.py`, `backend/models/schemas.py`)**: `compute_cluster_group_proportions()` now returns absolute `counts` alongside percentage `proportions`. `_apply_group_proportions()` populates `ClusterAnnotation.n_cells`. Report analysis log shows actual cell counts instead of placeholder text.
  - **Integration phase persistence fix (`backend/workflow/integration.py`)**: `analysis_phase` set to `INTEGRATION` before the gate/stop branch, ensuring the terminal phase is persisted even when the integration node short-circuits to the final report.
  - **Sandbox safety bypass for bioinfo wrappers (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: `execute_code`, `generate_publication_figure`, and auto-retry all use `skip_safety_check=True` since the code is already validated by the bioinformatics agent's own AST safety layer.
  - **New tests**: `test_secret_persistence.py` (credential encryption round-trip), `test_bioinformatics_evidence_gate.py` (evidence execution IDs), `test_annotation_cell_proportions.py` (counts field), `test_annotation_validation_callback_behavior.py` (absolute cluster counts), `test_bioinformatics_runtime_guards.py` (dense-matrix preflight), `test_config_masking.py` (masked preview preservation), `test_integration_synthesis_node.py` (phase persistence), `test_mechanism_handler_return.py` (evidence_execution_ids), `test_mechanism_program_validation.py` (missing evidence rejection), `test_mechanism_tool_schema.py` (schema field), `test_round_completion_status.py` (unverified evidence → partial), `test_session_config_routes.py` (masked preview round-trip).

- `0.34.10` — Bioinformatics executor checkpoint isolation, plan-revision context injection, planning context truncation; version bump (2026-07-21):
  - **Executor checkpoint isolation (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: New `_save_executor_checkpoint()` consolidates six inline checkpoint-save sites into one method. Checkpoint now includes `active_plan_id` for plan-identity tracking across resume. Plan-revision boundary triggers an immediate checkpoint (transcript + sandbox) instead of waiting for the periodic 5-step save. Pre-escalation checkpoints (empty-exhausted, text-only-exhausted) use distinct step names to prevent checkpoint-ID collisions.
  - **Plan-revision context injection on resume (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: New `build_approved_plan_context()` and `inject_revised_plan_context()` — on executor resume, if the current approved plan differs from the checkpoint's `active_plan_id`, the replacement plan is injected as a user message with revision reason and transition instructions. Legacy/rebuilt checkpoints (no plan ID) receive the plan once during migration. `_APPROVED_PLAN_PREFIX` tag enables downstream filtering.
  - **Planning context isolation (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: `generate_analysis_plan()` and `revise_plan_incrementally()` now use `chat_without_history()` to prevent planning transcripts from polluting the autonomous executor's message history. `_retry_on_parse_failure` retry uses `chat_without_history` for the same reason.
  - **Planning context truncation (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`, `backend/workflow/planning.py`)**: All planning prompts bounded via `truncate_for_llm()`: PI tasks (8K), overview (4K), previous results (4K), existing plan steps (12K), PI feedback (12K), user feedback (4K), data description (8K), execution revision context (4K each). Prevents unbounded prompt growth from multi-round planning dialogues.
  - **Execution-triggered revision feedback (`backend/workflow/planning.py`)**: When `plan_revision_needed` is set, the planning dialogue now includes `execution_revision_feedback` — the revision context and a bounded result summary — so the PI can make informed revision decisions without re-executing.
  - **Bioinformatics agent history reset on planning entry (`backend/workflow/planning.py`)**: `reset_history()` called on the bioinformatics agent before planning dialogue to clear legacy multi-round transcripts from in-memory sessions created before context isolation.
  - **New tests**: (covered by existing executor checkpoint and planning dialogue test suites).

- `0.34.8` — Planning dialogue stagnation detection, direction coverage eligibility, sandbox zstd compression, LLM log compaction, checkpoint SQLite pruning; version bump (2026-07-21):
  - **Planning dialogue stagnation detection (`backend/workflow/planning.py`)**: New `plans_materially_equivalent()` detects when a revision produces no material change via token similarity (≥0.97) and sequence similarity (≥0.94). When the dialogue converges without approval, PI finalizes unilaterally instead of looping to max rounds. New `finalization_reason` field (`no_material_plan_change` / `max_rounds`) in planning result.
  - **Direction coverage eligibility (`backend/workflow/synthesis.py`, `backend/workflow/direction_coverage.py`, `backend/workflow/routing.py`)**: New `RoundResult.direction_coverage_eligible` field distinguishes core-agent failure (ineligible) from optional-modality degradation (eligible). `completed_innovation_direction_keys()` uses the explicit field with backward-compatible fallback. New `attempted_innovation_direction_keys()` and `active_batch_has_unattempted_directions()` prevent unbounded directionless rounds when a batch is exhausted with incomplete evidence. `_check_convergence_condition` stops at partial-report gate instead of looping.
  - **Sandbox zstd compression (`backend/utils/sandbox.py`)**: `save_session` now writes streaming zstd-compressed dill frames (`SCAI_ZSTD_DILL_V1` magic header, level 1). `_restore_session` detects the magic header and decompresses transparently; legacy uncompressed streams remain readable. Reduces repetitive sparse/dataframe state from multi-GB raw files.
  - **Sandbox declared-output contract enforcement (`backend/utils/sandbox.py`)**: `execute()` now verifies every `persistent_outputs` name exists after successful execution; missing names set `result.success=False` with a descriptive error. `_prune_legacy_unobserved_required()` cleans up stale tracker entries from swallowed exceptions. `_record_globals_diff` only promotes confirmed (post-execution) declared names to required outputs.
  - **Sandbox AST safety rewrite (`backend/utils/sandbox.py`)**: Replaced regex-based dangerous builtin string detection with AST-based `_constant_string()` resolution and alias propagation. New `_dangerous_lookup_from_expr()` blocks `globals()["eval"]`, `builtins.__dict__["exec"]`, and `getattr(builtins, "compile")` patterns. Removed over-broad `__import__` word-boundary and string-referenced dangerous builtin regexes that caused false positives on normal column names.
  - **LLM log compaction (`backend/utils/llm_logger.py`)**: Session logs now compact messages (keep system + last 30, truncate text >8K chars, omit inline images), tools (first 30, truncate descriptions >1K), tool_calls (first 30, truncate args >8K), and response content (>100K chars). Daily logs are a lightweight operational index (message_count, response_chars, tool_names) instead of a second full copy. Prevents JSONL size from growing quadratically with conversation length.
  - **Checkpoint SQLite pruning (`backend/workflow/core.py`)**: New `_compact_checkpoints()` retains only the latest 5 checkpoints per namespace, prunes orphaned `writes` rows, clears dangling `parent_checkpoint_id`, runs `PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` and `VACUUM`. Called during workflow cleanup to prevent unbounded SQLite growth.
  - **Bioinformatics agent improvements (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: New `_build_resource_guard_preamble()` for scvelo/CellRank memory preflight. `_TPL_PLT_RESTORE_BASELINE` restores immutable plt baselines. `_build_publication_figure_wrapper()` unifies three drift-prone copies. Annotation validation uses single `adata_val.copy()` with `finally` release. `_upsert_publication_figure()` clears refreshed stems from `invalidated_artifact_stems`.
  - **Pathology tile validation (`backend/agents/pathology_agent.py`)**: New `_validate_tile_batch()` validates model-produced tile findings (correct count, non-empty, no placeholders) with up to 2 corrective attempts. `tile_validation` metadata attached to every image result. `_summarize_status()` returns "partial" for any error/skipped/partial image. Synthesis uses `chat_without_history` to avoid polluting message history.
  - **Painter 403 protocol-scoped (`backend/agents/painter_agent.py`)**: Bare 403 is no longer fatal — gateways may deny `/images/edits` while allowing chat image generation. `_is_fatal_image_error` only matches 401 with authentication keywords.
  - **PI agent direction normalization v2 (`backend/agents/pi_agent.py`)**: `_DIRECTION_CANDIDATE_LIST_FIELDS` expanded with `contradictory_or_missing_evidence`, `suggested_methods`, `risk_flags`. New `_normalize_supporting_data()` coerces scalar/string/list-of-list to documented shape. `_normalize_integration_scored_candidates()`: duplicate ID detection, filters empty values, preserves canonical raw `direction_id`. `_critical_direction_fields_missing()` rejects placeholders and enforces ≥2 alternatives.
  - **New tests**: `test_planning_dialogue_llm_failure.py`, `test_pathology_tile_validation.py`, `test_painter_retry.py` (403 protocol-scoped), `test_sandbox_dangerous_patterns.py` (AST safety), `test_sandbox_globals_diff_prune.py`, `test_sandbox_save_restore_streaming.py` (zstd + declared-output contract), `test_llm_logger_emit.py` (compaction), `test_direction_coverage.py`, `test_direction_evidence_models.py`, `test_integration_predicates.py`, `test_integration_routing.py`, `test_integration_convergence_routing.py`, `test_round_completion_status.py` (coverage eligibility), `test_workflow_runner.py`, `test_sandbox_failure_surfacing.py`.

- `0.34.7` — Responses API input shape sanitization, HITL gate payload in load response, default model refresh, literature mode defaults; version bump (2026-07-20):
  - **Responses API input shape sanitization (`backend/utils/llm_client.py`)**: New `_responses_messages_to_input()` strips null `tool_calls` and extra Chat Completions fields from assistant messages, rebuilding items to only include role/content for strict Responses API gateways. New `is_responses_input_shape_error()` classifies deterministic `input[]` schema errors as non-transient, preventing infinite retry loops on shape-mismatch 400s. `retry_with_backoff` breaks on these deterministic input-shape errors instead of retrying. Test files: `test_responses_adapter.py`, `test_retry_classification.py`, `test_transient_probe_classifier.py`.
  - **HITL gate payload in load response (`backend/models/schemas.py`, `frontend/src/types.ts`)**: `wait_input_reason`, `llm_failure_info`, `agent_disabled_info` now included in `AnalysisState.to_api_dict()` so HITL dialog state survives page reload instead of relying solely on best-effort WebSocket delivery.
  - **LLM failure state hydration on load (`frontend/src/utils/transformLoadResponse.ts`, `frontend/src/hooks/useApi.ts`)**: New `StoreUpdates.llmFailureInfo` rebuilt from authoritative `wait_input_reason`/`llm_failure_info`/`agent_disabled_info` fields. `applyStoreUpdates()` directly wires `llmFailureInfo` to store state and auto-opens the LLM timeout dialog, clearing stale state when no gate is pending.
  - **WebSocket reconnect for lost HITL gate (`frontend/src/components/AnalysisPanel.tsx`)**: When `waiting_input` status has no locally recoverable gate payload (exploration/feedback/integration/annotation data), explicitly calls `wsConnect()` to trigger backend re-emission of the pending state_sync.
  - **Default model refresh**: All agent default models updated: PI → `gpt-5.6-sol`, Pathology → `gemini-3.1-pro-preview`, Bioinformatics → `gpt-5.6-sol`, Biochemistry → `gemini-3.1-pro-preview`, Literature → `gpt-5.4-mini`, Painter → `gpt-image-2`, Supervisor → `gpt-5.4-mini`, Technique → `gpt-5.4-mini`. Updated across `backend/config/_config.py`, `backend/routes/session_config.py`, `frontend/src/stores/appStore.ts`.
  - **Literature mode default**: Changed from `deep_research` to `pubmed_llm` across all config layers (`_config.py`, `api_requests.py`, `config_schema.py`, `schemas.py`, `chat.py`, `core.py`, `config_io.py`, `appStore.ts`, `PromptsPanel.tsx`).
  - **Deep research model default**: Set to `o4-mini-deep-research` across all config layers.
  - **Max search rounds default**: Changed from 10 to 20 across all config layers.
  - **Frontend model recommendations**: Updated placeholder text and recommended model lists in `PromptsPanel.tsx` and `translations.ts` to reflect new model names (removed `google/` and `openai/` prefixes, added `deepseek-v4-*`, `glm-5.2`, `mimo-v2.5`; Technique agent recommendations added; placeholder uses `DEFAULT_MODELS[agent]`).
  - **New tests**: `test_agent_model_defaults.py` (model defaults alignment between backend and frontend, `test_to_api_dict_coverage.py` pending HITL gate fields), `appStore.defaults.test.ts` (frontend default values). `AnalysisPanel.integration-gate.test.tsx` (reconnect on waiting badge without gate payload).

- `0.34.6` — Expression contract system, artifact invalidation on representation change, sandbox save targets tracked deps, AST-based globals diff; version bump (2026-07-15):
  - **Expression contract system (`backend/utils/expression_contract.py`)**: New module providing AST-based detection of sensitive expression calls (DE, gene scoring, NMF), kernel-side preflight code that rejects raw integer count inputs, provenance tracking via `analysis_generation` in sandbox state, and artifact invalidation on representation change. `extract_sensitive_expression_inputs()` parses code for `rank_genes_groups`/`score_genes`/`score_genes_cell_cycle`/`non_negative_factorization`/NMF `fit_transform` calls. `build_expression_preflight_code()` generates kernel code that samples matrix values, detects raw counts vs normalized continuous vs centered/scaled, and raises `ValueError` on violation. `mutates_expression_representation()` detects `normalize_total`/`log1p`/`scale`/`normalize_per_cell` or `.X` assignment. `record_representation_change()` bumps generation and invalidates same-round artifacts. `workspace_artifact_versions()`/`unchanged_artifact_stems()`/`record_invalidated_file_stems()` track stale derived files.
  - **Artifact validity system (`backend/services/report/artifacts.py`)**: New module with `is_report_eligible()` (checks `is_current`, `invalidated_at`, `review_status`), `eligible_artifacts()`, `artifact_stems()`, and `invalidate_current_artifacts()` for marking stale figures/tables.
  - **PublicationFigure/PublicationTable schema extensions (`backend/models/schemas.py`)**: New fields: `round_number`, `analysis_generation`, `expression_provenance`, `is_current`, `invalidated_at`, `invalidation_reason`. New `AnalysisState.invalidated_artifact_stems` list.
  - **Bioinformatics agent integration (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: `execute_code` and `generate_publication_figure` both run expression preflight before sensitive calls. `record_expression_contract()` stores contract in sandbox state. `record_representation_change()` bumps generation and invalidates stale artifacts. `_upsert_publication_figure()` clears refreshed stems from `invalidated_artifact_stems`. `count_saved_figures()` deduplicates by stem and counts `pub_figure_name`.
  - **Supervisor agent artifact filtering (`backend/agents/supervisor_agent.py`)**: `_collect_figure_paths` and `_collect_table_paths` filter out invalidated artifacts. Report context uses `eligible_artifacts()` for publication figures.
  - **Report path artifact filtering (`backend/services/report/variants.py`, `backend/services/report/generator.py`, `backend/services/report/analysis_log.py`, `backend/services/report/figures.py`, `backend/workflow/reporting.py`)**: All report generators filter figures/tables through `eligible_artifacts()` and `invalidated_artifact_stems`. Detailed report segments scoped to current round's analysis log and figures. New `_deduplicate_detailed_figure_blocks()` removes duplicate keyed figures across combined report segments.
  - **Sandbox save targets tracked deps (`backend/utils/sandbox.py`)**: `save_session` now targets `CANONICAL_RUNTIME_ARTIFACTS` + `get_runtime_dependency_names()` instead of sweeping all globals for AnnData objects. Uses `__SCAI_ALIAS__` tagged three-item frames for object aliases (avoids serializing the same object twice). Failed required-variable save raises `RuntimeError` instead of logging a warning. `_last_save_skipped` populated on both success and failure paths.
  - **AST-based globals diff (`backend/utils/sandbox.py`)**: `_loaded_global_names_from_code()` uses AST to extract statically-read names. `_record_globals_diff()` uses this instead of treating the full before/after intersection as "read", preventing promotion of untouched scratch variables.
  - **Importlib safety rewrite (`backend/utils/sandbox.py`)**: `_is_safe_importlib()` rewritten with AST to allow only `importlib.import_module`/`reload`/`util.find_spec`/`metadata.version`/`metadata.packages_distributions`. Blocks submodules like `importlib.machinery`.
  - **Compile pattern fix (`backend/utils/sandbox.py`)**: `compile` dangerous pattern changed from `\bcompile\s*\(` to `(?<![\w.])compile\s*\(` to allow `re.compile(...)`.
  - **Numba cache isolation (`backend/utils/sandbox.py`)**: Numba cache dir uses session-scoped temp dir (`_session_tmp`) instead of shared `/tmp/numba_cache___SESSION_ID__`.
  - **Dependency addition (`requirements.txt`)**: Added `scikit-misc>=0.3.1,<0.5.0` (required by `scanpy.pp.highly_variable_genes(flavor="seurat_v3")`).
  - **New tests**: `test_sandbox_dangerous_patterns.py` (re.compile allowed, safe importlib, unsafe importlib blocked), `test_sandbox_globals_diff_prune.py` (only loaded names promoted), `test_sandbox_save_restore_streaming.py` (tracked deps save, incomplete required save raises), `test_sandbox_write_boundary.py` (numba cache inside session tmp), `test_bioinformatics_figure_count_log.py` (pub_figure_name dedup), `test_report_variants.py` (deduplicate detailed figure blocks).

- `0.34.5` — Pathology tile validation & HITL safety, unified direction selection history across all report paths, round completion status for stop-and-summarize, LLM logger single-write fix; version bump (2026-07-15):
  - **Pathology tile validation (`backend/agents/pathology_agent.py`)**: New `_validate_tile_batch()` validates model-produced tile findings at the batch level: enforces correct count, non-empty findings (>12 chars), rejects placeholders and generic responses. Up to 2 validation attempts per batch with corrective feedback. `tile_validation` metadata (status, coverage ratio, invalid batches) attached to every image result. `_summarize_status()` now returns "partial" when any image has error/skipped/partial status. Synthesis and overview use `chat_without_history` to avoid polluting message history.
  - **Pathology skip/terminate propagation (`backend/agents/pathology_agent.py`)**: `_ImageSkipRequested` and `WorkflowTerminatedError` now cross the generic exception boundary in `_analyze_image` and `targeted_analysis` instead of being swallowed as per-image errors. Targeted analysis records skip with explicit `status: "skipped"` and no `error` field.
  - **Unified direction selection history (`backend/services/report/interaction_records.py`)**: New `build_direction_selection_history()` iterates all `DirectionSelectionRecord` entries (pi_decide→PI-selected, select→User-selected, skips open/finalize), falls back to legacy `user_chosen_directions`. New `render_direction_selection_history_html()` renders a 4-column table (Selection Event, Decision Mode, Direction, Notes) reused across all report paths.
