  - **All report paths use shared direction history (`backend/services/report/sections.py`, `backend/services/report/generator.py`, `backend/services/report/regenerator.py`, `backend/agents/supervisor_agent.py`)**: Supervisor, detailed report generator, regenerator, and section builder all use the shared `build_direction_selection_history()` / `render_direction_selection_history_html()` instead of inline `user_chosen_directions` processing.
  - **Round completion status for stop-and-summarize (`backend/workflow/synthesis.py`)**: `_statuses_for_keys()` accepts `missing_status` parameter; absent core agent result keys return "not_run". `derive_round_completion_status` uses `missing_status="not_run"` for core agents — missing results → partial (if evidence exists) or skipped. `_summarize_agent_results` treats absent keys as `{"status": "not_run", "findings": []}`.
  - **LLM logger single-write fix (`backend/utils/llm_client.py`)**: `_emit_log_response()` now calls async logger exclusively when available, falling back to sync only when async is missing. Fixes double-write where both `log_response` and `log_response_async` logged simultaneously.
  - **New tests**: `test_pathology_llm_failure_hitl.py` (skip/terminate through `_analyze_image`), `test_pathology_targeted_hitl.py` (skip recording, terminate propagation), `test_pathology_soft_failure_status.py` (partial status semantics, tile coverage partial), `test_interaction_records_render.py` (direction history all records + legacy fallback), `test_supervisor_report_shared.py` (complete direction history in appendix), `test_regenerator_appendix.py` (enriched direction history), `test_round_completion_status.py` (missing core → partial/skipped), `test_llm_logger_emit.py` (sync-only fallback, daily+session write count). Extended: `test_llm_logger_async.py` (monkeypatch for session dir).

- `0.34.4` — pip build-constraint support for PEP 517 build isolation, setuptools compatibility pin; version bump (2026-07-15):
  - **pip build-constraint support (`docker-entrypoint.sh`, `start.sh`)**: Both Docker and `start.sh` now probe pip for `--build-constraint` support (pip ≥25.3) and apply `build-constraints.txt` inside isolated PEP 517 build environments. Older pip versions use the `PIP_CONSTRAINT` env var instead. `docker-entrypoint.sh` additionally respects `$PIP_INDEX_URL` for custom package indexes. All ``PIP_CONSTRAINT`` / ``--build-constraint`` references are driven by an opt-in `build-constraints.txt` that users create only when they need it, keeping the default install path unchanged.
  - **setuptools compatibility pin (`requirements.txt`)**: Added `setuptools<82` to prevent legacy scientific packages (e.g. numba, llvmlite, scipy ecosystem) that use `pkg_resources` from breaking under setuptools ≥82 where `pkg_resources` is no longer bundled.

- `0.34.3` — Bioinformatics resource preflight, publication-figure wrapper unification, plt baseline restore, direction candidate normalization v2, Painter 403 protocol-scoped, pin scientific stack, annotation validation single-copy; version bump (2026-07-15):
  - **Bioinformatics resource preflight (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: AST-based detection of scvelo velocity (`scv.tl.velocity`/`velocity_graph`) and CellRank calls. `_build_resource_guard_preamble()` generates kernel-side memory measurement code that computes AnnData matrix bytes (X/layers/obsp/varp/obsm/varm, including sparse parts), reads `/proc/meminfo` and cgroup limits, and raises `MemoryError` with actionable guidance when the estimated budget exceeds 70% of available headroom. Prevents silent OOM-killed kernel during velocity/fate analysis on large datasets.
  - **plt baseline restore (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: New `_TPL_PLT_RESTORE_BASELINE` restores `plt.close`/`plt.show`/`Figure.savefig` to immutable baselines captured at kernel init (`_original_plt_close`, `_capturing_show`/`_original_plt_show`, `_combined_savefig`), instead of trusting the mutable `plt` alias that user code may rebind. Both `_build_exec_autosave_wrapper` and `_build_publication_figure_wrapper` use it.
  - **Publication-figure wrapper unification (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: New `_build_publication_figure_wrapper()` replaces three inline drift-prone copies (initial render, revision, auto-retry). All three paths now share one JPG+PDF save template that includes the resource guard preamble and baseline restore.
  - **Annotation validation single-copy (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: Single `adata_val = var_name.copy()` at loop start instead of per-iteration. `finally` block releases via `globals().pop('adata_val', None)` to free large AnnData copies promptly. Timeout default 180s → 600s. Prompt updated to reflect single-copy pattern.
  - **Direction candidate normalization v2 (`backend/agents/pi_agent.py`)**: `_DIRECTION_CANDIDATE_LIST_FIELDS` expanded with `contradictory_or_missing_evidence`, `suggested_methods`, `risk_flags`. New `_normalize_supporting_data()` coerces scalar/string/list-of-list supporting_data to documented `[{source, evidence, strength}]` shape. `_normalize_integration_scored_candidates()`: duplicate ID detection (falls back to direction name), filters empty values on merge, preserves canonical raw `direction_id`. `generate_raw_direction_candidates` and `score_directions_with_evidence` both return normalized data. `_critical_direction_fields_missing()` rejects placeholders ("tbd", "n/a", "..."), enforces ≥2 meaningful alternatives, and validates `validation_plan` sub-fields.
  - **Painter 403 protocol-scoped (`backend/agents/painter_agent.py`)**: `_is_client_error` → `_is_response_format_compat_error` (only 400/422). Bare 403 is no longer fatal or client-scoped — gateways may deny `/images/edits` while allowing chat image generation. `_is_fatal_image_error` only matches 401 with authentication keywords and content-moderation keywords.
  - **Centralized `as_list()` (`backend/utils/formatting.py`)**: New `as_list()` utility replaces local copies in `interaction_records.py`. Prevents character-by-character string iteration in report rendering. Used by pi_agent, literature_agent, interaction_records, variants.
  - **Literature agent scalar field safety (`backend/agents/literature_agent.py`)**: `_candidate_terms` uses centralized `as_list()` for key_genes/key_cell_types/key_pathways/suggested_methods.
  - **Sandbox file-write AST safety (`backend/utils/sandbox.py`)**: `_is_safe_file_write` replaced line-by-line regex with AST analysis. Only literal unsafe paths are rejected statically; computed/aliased paths pass to the runtime `_sandboxed_open` allowlist.
  - **Pin scientific stack (`requirements.txt`)**: numpy==1.26.4, numba==0.61.2, llvmlite==0.44.0, scanpy==1.11.5, anndata==0.11.4, scvelo==0.3.3, cellrank==2.0.7, decoupler==1.6.0, scvi-tools==1.3.3.
  - **Config update (`backend/config/_config.py`, `backend/workflow/annotation.py`)**: `annotation_validation_timeout_seconds` default 180 → 600.
  - **New tests**: `test_painter_retry.py` (generic 403 protocol-scoped, 403 not fatal), `test_annotation_validation_executor.py` (single copy, timeout release), `test_direction_literature_triage.py` (scalar candidate terms), `test_pi_score_field_contract.py` (normalized fields, name-fallback preserves canonical ID, no raw-list aliasing, duplicate raw IDs, round1 raw normalization, placeholder/incomplete-plan detection). Extended: `test_interaction_records_render.py` (full scalar field rendering), `test_report_variants.py` (scalar list render), `test_annotation_validation_config.py` (timeout default 600).

- `0.34.2` — Direction candidate field normalization, scalar-to-list coercion in LLM responses and report rendering; version bump (2026-07-15):
  - **Direction candidate field normalization (`backend/agents/pi_agent.py`)**: New `_normalize_direction_candidate()` coerces unstable LLM field shapes for integration direction candidates: `supporting_data` → array of objects, all list-typed fields (`alternative_hypotheses`, `artifact_risks`, `key_genes`, `key_cell_types`, `key_pathways`, `key_uncertainties`, `suggested_literature_topics`, `suggested_validation_analysis`, `suggested_wet_lab_validation`, `decision_criteria`, `prerequisite_dependencies`) → arrays of strings. `validation_plan` sub-fields (`next_analysis`, `wet_lab_validation`, `decision_criteria`) coalesced from both `validation_plan` and flat `suggested_*` fields. New `_normalize_integration_scored_candidates()` merges score-only LLM responses onto their raw candidates before normalization, preventing data loss during integration scoring. Both `generate_integration_directions` and `score_integration_directions_with_evidence` now return normalized data.
  - **Prompt type specification (`backend/agents/pi_agent.py`)**: Updated `generate_integration_directions` prompt to specify exact JSON types (arrays of strings, `supporting_data` as array of objects with `source`/`evidence`/`strength` fields). Example field shapes included in the prompt.
  - **Scalar-to-list coercion in report rendering (`backend/services/report/interaction_records.py`)**: New `_as_list()` helper in interaction records rendering treats scalar single-string values as one-item lists instead of iterating character-by-character. Applied to `supporting_data`, `alternative_hypotheses`, `artifact_risks`, `contradictory_or_missing_evidence`, and `validation_plan` sub-fields. Fixes HTML output where a string like `"Round 2 and 3 share an NRF2 signal."` rendered as individual characters `<li>R</li><li>o</li><li>u</li>…`.
  - **New tests**: `test_interaction_records_render.py` (scalar list fields rendered as single items). Extended: `test_pi_score_field_contract.py` (normalized integration direction shapes, scoring merge preserves raw fields).

- `0.34.1` — Snapshot V2 disabled-by-default feature gate, post-exploration phase drift repair, frontend V2 UI gating; version bump (2026-07-15):
  - **Snapshot V2 disabled-by-default (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/feature_gate.py`, `backend/config/_config.py`, `backend/config/__init__.py`)**: New `SCAI_ENABLE_SNAPSHOT_V2` environment variable (`1`/`true`/`yes`, case-insensitive) controls the process-scoped feature gate. Default is disabled. `is_snapshot_v2_enabled()` and `SNAPSHOT_V2_ENABLED` exported from `backend.config`. `require_snapshot_v2_enabled()` FastAPI dependency returns HTTP 404 with `SNAPSHOT_V2_DISABLED` for all V2 HTTP routes. `normalize_disabled_snapshot_state()` clears stale snapshot blockers (`snapshot_failure`, `pending_required_snapshot`, `wait_input_reason`, `skip_required_snapshot_phase`) and downgrades phantom RUNNING/WAITING_INPUT to PAUSED. Test conftest sets `SCAI_ENABLE_SNAPSHOT_V2=true` to keep existing snapshot regression tests unchanged.
  - **Backend HTTP route gating (`backend/routes/snapshots.py`, `backend/routes/lifecycle.py`, `backend/routes/export_import.py`, `backend/routes/chat.py`, `backend/routes/config.py`)**: All Snapshot V2 HTTP endpoints (list/get/delete/restore/transaction status/cancel, snapshot-failure retry/skip/terminate, V2 export/import) gated with `Depends(require_snapshot_v2_enabled)`. Post-chat snapshot capture no-ops when disabled. `/config/status` returns `snapshot_v2_enabled` boolean for frontend capability detection.
  - **Startup and session-load gating (`backend/main.py`, `backend/services/session_store.py`)**: Lifespan journal recovery only runs when enabled. `ensure_session_loaded` runs disabled-mode state normalization on cached and disk-loaded sessions; V2 manifest reconciliation and recovery-blocked paths only run when enabled.
  - **Workflow/Report gating (`backend/workflow/core.py`, `backend/workflow/reporting.py`)**: `_capture_required_snapshot` no-ops when disabled. `run`, `generate_final_report`, `resume_workflow_thread` call `normalize_disabled_snapshot_state` before proceeding. Post-report capture extracted to `_capture_post_report_if_enabled` and skipped when disabled; `COMPLETED` status set directly without snapshot dependency.
  - **Post-exploration phase drift repair (`backend/routes/interaction.py`)**: `_choose_direction_locked` repairs `analysis_phase` from `EXPLORATION` to `INNOVATION` when a completed exploration report exists with direction candidates and no user choice yet. Fixes snapshot-restore scenario where phase=EXPLORATION persisted despite Report A being complete.
  - **Frontend Snapshot V2 feature gating (`frontend/src/stores/appStore.ts`, `frontend/src/hooks/useApi.ts`, `frontend/src/components/AnalysisPanel.tsx`)**: New `snapshotV2Enabled` store state (default false) + `setSnapshotV2Enabled`. `checkStatus` reads `snapshot_v2_enabled` from backend; `useConfig` wires through. AnalysisPanel: all Snapshot V2 UI (Snapshot button in all status sections, SnapshotFailureDialog, SnapshotPanel, CleanupReminder, RuntimeWarningBanner, transaction polling, auto-open on retry, cleanup state reset) gated behind `snapshotV2Enabled`. When disabled, no V2 API calls are made and stale V2 UI state is cleaned up.
  - **README doc update (`README.md`)**: Snapshot V2 safety contract section updated with opt-in mechanism (`SCAI_ENABLE_SNAPSHOT_V2=true`) and disabled-behavior description.
  - **New tests**: `test_choose_direction_gating.py` (post-exploration phase drift repair). Frontend: `AnalysisPanel.snapshot-failure-retry.test.tsx` (hides Snapshot V2 UI when disabled), `appStore.snapshot-v2.test.ts` (default disabled, update capability).

- `0.33.18` — Legacy snapshot-failure gate upgrade on reconnect; version bump (2026-07-15):
  - **Legacy gate upgrade on reconnect (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/failure_gate.py`, `backend/routes/websockets.py`)**: New `upgrade_legacy_snapshot_failure_gate()` upgrades persisted pre-producer snapshot failure gates in place during WebSocket reconnect. Bare gates (no `retry_context.producer`) are upgraded to typed gates: workflow boundary (for known phases or unknown/manual), post-report (when `pending_required_snapshot` is set or phase is `post_report`). Pre_restore gates with explicit typed contexts remain untouched. Fixes a scenario where a restart/reconnect left the frontend with a Retry/Manage/Terminate-only gate for a skippable producer.
  - **New tests**: `test_snapshot_failure_resume_gate.py` (`test_ws_resend_upgrades_legacy_bare_snapshot_failure_gate`), `test_snapshot_failure_skip.py` (legacy bare gate upgraded to skippable workflow boundary, typed pre_restore gate preserved).

- `0.33.17` — Pre-restore quota grace, typed snapshot failure gate with immediate skippable actions, content-aware deduplicated prewrite estimate; version bump (2026-07-15):
  - **Pre-restore quota grace (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/service.py`, `backend/config/_config.py`)**: New `snapshot_v2_pre_restore_quota_grace_bytes` (default 64MB) allows pre-restore captures to proceed when the cumulative object store already exceeds the session quota baseline, bounded by a configurable grace ceiling. Non-pre-restore captures are unaffected. `SnapshotService` constructor, `_check_quota`, and `_estimate_capture_addition` all updated.
  - **Content-aware deduplicated prewrite estimate (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/service.py`)**: `_estimate_capture_addition` now hashes workspace files, notebook, checkpoint, and state against existing object-store SHAs, counting only new bytes. Eliminates over-counting when retrying a capture whose objects are already on disk.
  - **Typed snapshot failure gate with immediate skippable actions (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/service.py`, `backend/services/snapshot_v2/failure_gate.py`)**: `capture_required_snapshot` now calls `ensure_workflow_boundary_retry_context()` before emitting the state_sync, so the first failure event carries typed `retry_context` (producer, resume_policy, snapshot_request phase). Workflow-boundary, post-report, and chat failures immediately advertise Skip in `allowed_actions` instead of requiring a second sync.
  - **Frontend snapshot failure action resolution (`frontend/src/utils/snapshotV2.ts`, `frontend/src/stores/appStore.ts`, `frontend/src/hooks/useApi.ts`)**: New `resolveSnapshotFailureActions()` infers Skip for known skippable producers (`workflow_boundary`, `post_report`, `chat`) based on `retry_context.producer`; `pre_restore` and unknown producers remain fail-closed (retry/manage/terminate only). Used by WebSocket handler, `setSnapshotFailure` store action, and `parseSnapshotFailure`.
  - **Snapshot panel modal layer ownership (`frontend/src/components/AnalysisPanel.tsx`)**: `SnapshotFailureDialog` hidden when `SnapshotPanel` is open, preventing double-modal layout. Failure dialog reappears when the snapshot panel closes, preserving gate state.
  - **Lifecycle generic-resume message update (`backend/routes/lifecycle.py`)**: Message broadened to reference "snapshot-failure action" instead of specific action names, staying correct as action sets vary by producer.
  - **Structural boundary test updated (`backend/tests/test_snapshot_v2_structural_boundary.py`)**: Replaced `test_no_skip_in_snapshot_v2_failure_ux_comments` with `test_snapshot_v2_failure_skip_is_producer_aware` that verifies skippable producers are documented and pre_restore remains fail-closed.
  - **Doc updates**: AGENTS.md Snapshot V2 section updated to reflect skippable producers; README.md fail-closed table updated.
  - **New tests**: `test_snapshot_failure_gate.py` (`test_required_workflow_boundary_emits_skippable_typed_gate_immediately`), `test_snapshot_v2_quota_accounting.py` (deduplicated prewrite estimate, pre_restore grace acceptance, pre_restore grace rejection). Frontend: `AnalysisPanel.snapshot-failure-retry.test.tsx` (snapshot manager modal layer ownership), `appStore.snapshot-v2.test.ts` (Skip hydration from workflow-boundary retry context, pre_restore exclusion).

- `0.33.16` — Quiescent session restore safe-point acknowledgement, post-report dead-sandbox bypass; version bump (2026-07-15):
  - **Quiescent session restore safe-point ack (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/jobs.py`)**: New `_acknowledge_if_quiescent()` atomically checks (under `state_lock`) that a session has no live workflow, no run/report/chat task, and no ongoing initialization. When the session is fully quiescent, acknowledges the restore safe point with `role="quiescent_coordinator"` instead of waiting for a workflow boundary handshake that may never arrive. Fixes a regression where completed/failed report workflows left the session in `waiting_safe_point` until timeout.
  - **Post-report dead-sandbox bypass (`backend/workflow/reporting.py`)**: `_capture_post_report_snapshot` now checks `sandbox.km is not None` and routes through the trusted terminal/no-kernel path when the kernel is gone, instead of re-declaring historical runtime dependencies that require reading from a nonexistent kernel.
  - **Safe point doc update (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/safe_point.py`)**: `acknowledge_safe_point_request` docstring clarifies that `role="quiescent_coordinator"` (alongside `role="workflow"`) never requires the requester token.
  - **New tests**: `test_snapshot_v2_restore_jobs.py` (`test_quiescent_session_restore_does_not_wait_for_workflow_ack`, `test_initializing_session_restore_still_waits_for_boundary_ack`), `test_snapshot_v2_producers.py` (`test_post_report_dead_sandbox_uses_empty_complete_path`). Extended: `TestCommittedJobLiveSideEffects` registers a mock workflow to avoid the new quiescent path.

- `0.33.15` — Integration gate accessible modal dialog, focus management & keyboard trap, countdown-preserving close/reopen; version bump (2026-07-15):
  - **Integration gate accessible modal dialog (`frontend/src/components/IntegrationGatePanel.tsx`)**: Converted from a flat panel to an accessible modal: `role="dialog"`, `aria-modal="true"`, `aria-labelledby`, close button with close `aria-label` and `X` icon (lucide-react). Backdrop overlay (`fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm`). Visual redesign with `max-h-[90vh]` scrollable container, rounded-2xl card, header/footer borders, slate-50 footer background. Added `open` and `onClose` props for controlled visibility.
  - **Focus management & keyboard trap**: Auto-focuses close button on open via `data-autofocus`; restores `previousFocusRef` on close. Tab/Shift+Tab cycles among enabled focusable controls (excluding disabled/submitting buttons). Escape key closes the dialog without resolving the gate. Focus restoration on authoritative unmount (e.g. stop/resume clears session status).
  - **Controlled dialog visibility in AnalysisPanel (`frontend/src/components/AnalysisPanel.tsx`)**: New `showIntegrationGateDialog` state decouples dialog visibility from `integrationGateData`. Dialog opens when data arrives (`setShowIntegrationGateDialog(true)`), closes on any action (submit, finalize, pi-decide) or explicit close. "Waiting for Input" badge re-opens by setting `showIntegrationGateDialog=true` without re-invoking the action. Stale-gate guard clears both state variables.
  - **Countdown-preserving close/reopen**: The original countdown deadline continues running while the modal is closed; reopening shows the remaining time. No-wait auto-PI-decide fires only once regardless of close/reopen cycles.
  - **New tests**: `AnalysisPanel.integration-gate.test.tsx` (auto-open + close/reopen from badge, countdown preserved across close/reopen, no-repeat no-wait PI decision, focus restore on authoritative unmount). `IntegrationGate.test.tsx` (accessible modal rendering, focus trap forward/backward, Escape close with focus restoration, focus trap during submission, focus restoration on successful unmount).

- `0.33.14` — Snapshot V2 lineage resolution, safe-point acquire hardening, dill residual integrity, V1 management route hardening, quota enforcement & cleanup; version bump (2026-07-15):
  - **Lineage resolution for all V2 producers (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/lineage.py`, `backend/workflow/core.py`, `backend/workflow/reporting.py`, `backend/routes/chat.py`)**: Shared `capture_lineage_from_state()` resolves `parent_id`, `branch_id`, `branch_label` from `AnalysisState` for every forward capture. Workflow, chat, and report capture requests now consistently pass lineage metadata and `safe_point_owner_token`.
  - **Safe-point acquire returns bool (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/safe_point.py`)**: `_acquire_unlocked()` now checks `_pending_requests` for proper double-book prevention. `acknowledge_safe_point_request()` propagates acquire failure and re-queues the request instead of silently returning False. `is_owned_by()` added for owner-token verification.
  - **Safe-point owner token propagation (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/service.py`, `backend/routes/chat.py`)**: `capture_required_snapshot` / `capture` accept `safe_point_owner_token`; `_acquire_safe_point_for_capture` raises `SAFE_POINT_BUSY` when the safe point is held by another owner.
  - **Active-workflow state guard (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/service.py`)**: `_has_trusted_workflow_snapshot_context` rejects captures from an active workflow that has no observable graph state and no safe-point descriptor, returning a typed `LIVE_WORKFLOW_STATE_UNAVAILABLE` failure instead of silently capturing an empty state.
  - **Workflow state accessor convention (`backend/workflow/core.py`)**: `get_snapshot_workflow_state()` returns `copy.deepcopy()` of `_current_graph_state`. Service layer `_workflow_state_for_snapshot()` prefers the explicit accessor with a private-attr fallback for test doubles.
  - **Previous manifest resolution (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/service.py`)**: `_resolve_previous_manifest()` loads and verifies the lineage-head manifest declared by `AnalysisState.last_snapshot_id`; a declared-but-missing head raises `SnapshotIntegrityError` instead of silently continuing with no parent.
  - **Pre-capture quota estimate (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/service.py`)**: `_estimate_capture_addition()` conservatively counts known workspace files, notebook, checkpoint, and serialized state bytes before any object is written, enabling quota-rejection before I/O.
  - **Cumulative object-store quota enforcement (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/service.py`)**: `_physical_object_store_bytes()` sums all object files; `_check_quota` verifies cumulative size against the session quota, preventing storage overflow from repeated retries with identical content.
  - **Cleanup on capture failure (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/service.py`)**: `_cleanup_unpublished_capture_objects()` runs after a failed manifest publish, removing only objects created during the current transaction that are unreachable from committed snapshots (uses `SnapshotGarbageCollector.mark()` as proof).
  - **Partial-apply failure helper (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/service.py`)**: `_partial_apply_failure()` sets `recovery_blocked=True`, marks `PAUSED` status, and adds the session to `_recovery_blocked_sessions`, used by both `APPLY_FAILED` and `CANCELLED_AFTER_APPLYING` paths.
  - **Dill residual integrity (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/runtime.py`)**: Template truncates failed dill frames by seeking back to the pre-write checkpoint to prevent leak of partial object state. New in-kernel readback verification (`_runtime_dill.load` loop + `__DILL_VERIFIED_NAMES__`) confirms saved==verified; mismatch removes the file and marks every name as skipped. Host-side `_capture_residual_dill` rejects successful execution that lacks structural saved/skipped metadata output, preventing false-positive capture from legacy or truncated kernel output.
  - **ObjectStore fsync hardening (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/object_store.py`)**: Replaced `Path.write_bytes` + `os.replace` with `tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile`, explicit `os.fsync`, and `os.replace` for atomic durable write. Added `invalidate_evidence()` for cleanup-triggered cache dropping. New `load_verified_including_pending()` and `load_pending_verified()` methods for GC and import integrity verification.
  - **Dispatcher continuation gate-payload clearing (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/dispatcher.py`)**: `_clear_gate_payload()` strips `restore_gate_payload` from sandbox state on restore. Idle restore clears all `restore_resume_*` fields instead of leaving them for a stale automatic restart. Terminal restore also clears gate payload.
  - **Preflight runtime-choice semantics corrected (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/preflight.py`)**: `runtime_choice_required` is now derived solely from `runtime_status != "complete"` (not from payload presence). Workflow-state JSON validation moved before status determination so corrupt content correctly sets `compatibility_errors` and the snapshot is classified `corrupted`.
  - **V1 management route hardening (`backend/routes/snapshots.py`)**: `_require_v1_snapshot()` rejects V2 compatibility projections at legacy management routes with HTTP 409. `delete_branch` checks every descendant for V2 status. `delete_snapshot` rejects empty `state_file`. `format_version` normalisation extracted to `normalize_snapshot_format_version()` in `legacy.py`.
  - **Import ID collision idempotency (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/importer.py`)**: Same snapshot ID with identical content is idempotent (reuses committed root); same ID with different content raises `IMPORT_SNAPSHOT_ID_COLLISION`.
  - **Deletion export-protection hardening (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/deletion.py`)**: When export-protection records cannot be loaded, raises `SnapshotDeleteConflict` with `EXPORT_PROTECTION_UNAVAILABLE` instead of silently continuing.
  - **Cancel-after-applying in restore jobs (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/jobs.py`)**: Cancel while status is `applying` sets the cancel event but does NOT release the safe point; the service returns `CANCELLED_AFTER_APPLYING` with `recovery_required=True`.
  - **Canonical notebook path (`backend/utils/live_paths.py`, `backend/utils/sandbox.py`, `backend/services/report/analysis_log.py`, `backend/services/report/generator.py`)**: New `live_notebook_path()` in `live_paths.py` centralises the session notebook path. Sandbox `NotebookState.notebook_path`, analysis log, and report generator all use it, eliminating path-construction duplication.
  - **Quarantine dedup (`backend/utils/runtime_apply.py`)**: Repeated `quarantine_live_runtime()` appends `.1`, `.2` suffixes instead of silently overwriting earlier quarantine evidence.
  - **New tests**: `test_snapshot_v2_pre_restore.py` (lineage resolution tests: `test_restore_service_resolves_verified_lineage_head`, `test_restore_service_rejects_missing_declared_lineage_head`, `test_restore_context_wires_resolved_previous_manifest`), `test_snapshot_v2_producers.py` (test_normal_v2_producers_use_shared_lineage_resolution, `test_capture_lineage_defaults_and_restored_branch_values`, `test_workflow_snapshot_state_accessor_returns_deep_copy`, `test_restore_service_prefers_explicit_workflow_snapshot_accessor`, `test_active_workflow_requires_snapshot_state_or_safe_point_descriptor`), `test_snapshot_v2_runtime.py` (test_save_dill_template_rolls_back_failed_frame, `test_residual_capture_rejects_missing_structural_output`), `test_snapshot_v2_runtime_h5ad_restore.py` (test_empty_complete_runtime_removes_stale_residual, `test_repeated_quarantine_preserves_prior_runtime`), `test_snapshot_v2_quota_accounting.py` (test_prewrite_estimate_counts_known_workspace_and_state_artifacts, `test_quota_failure_cannot_be_bypassed_by_identical_retry`), `test_snapshot_v2_restore_jobs.py` (test_partial_apply_failure_helper_blocks_current_session, `test_cancel_after_applying_is_recovery_required`), `test_snapshot_rollback.py` (test_v1_delete_rejects_empty_state_file). Extended: `test_snapshot_v2_producers.py`, `test_chat_snapshot_safety.py`, `test_snapshot_v2_preflight.py`, `test_snapshot_v2_restore_transaction.py`, `test_snapshot_v2_restore_acceptance.py`, `test_snapshot_v2_continuation_restore.py`, `integration/test_snapshot_v2_generation_consistency.py`, `test_snapshot_v2_deletion.py`, `test_snapshot_v2_gc.py`, `test_snapshot_v2_import.py`, `test_snapshot_v2_object_store.py`, `test_snapshot_v2_safe_point.py`, `test_snapshot_v2_api.py`, `test_snapshot_v1_cutover.py`.

- `0.33.13` — Annotation gate survive reload, route registry FastAPI compatibility, concurrency safe-point fencing; version bump (2026-07-15):
  - **Annotation gate survive reload (`frontend/src/utils/annotationGate.ts`, `frontend/src/types.ts`, `frontend/src/stores/appStore.ts`, `frontend/src/hooks/useApi.ts`, `frontend/src/utils/transformLoadResponse.ts`, `frontend/src/components/AnalysisPanel.tsx`, `backend/models/schemas.py`)**: The cell-annotation confirmation dialog previously relied solely on the transient `annotation_confirmation` realtime message, which was lossy (500-message replay window, 1000-message stream cap, snapshot restore drops it). Now `annotation_state` is exposed via `AnalysisState.to_api_dict()` and carried on `/load`. New `buildAnnotationDataFromState()` in `utils/annotationGate.ts` rebuilds the `AnnotationData` dialog payload from the server's authoritative state. The store holds `annotationGateData` (server-authoritative, not persisted). On reconnect/reload, `AnalysisPanel.handleReopenDialog()` and the WebSocket message handler fall back to `annotationGateData` when the gate message is absent. Close button gets an `aria-label`.
  - **Route registry FastAPI compatibility (`backend/tests/test_route_registry.py`)**: Replaced `hasattr(r, "path")` enumeration with `_iter_routes()` that expands FastAPI's `_IncludedRouter` wrapper (introduced in FastAPI ~0.13x). Added `test_route_enumeration_sees_the_mounted_routers` guard (asserts `> 50` routes reachable) to prevent silent regression.
  - **Concurrency safe-point fencing (`backend/tests/integration/test_snapshot_v2_concurrency.py`)**: `test_job_manager_concurrent_enqueue` updated for safe-point reservation: a second restore enqueue now raises `SAFE_POINT_BUSY` (HTTP 409) instead of queueing alongside the first. Added `monkeypatch` for fast timeout.
  - **Backend minor**: `to_api_dict` coverage test un-gates `annotation_state`, gates `skip_required_snapshot_phase`. `AnnotationConfirmationDialog` close button gains `aria-label`.
  - **New tests**: `backend/tests/test_annotation_gate_reload.py` (annotation state exposure contract), `frontend/src/utils/annotationGate.test.ts` (`buildAnnotationDataFromState` unit), `frontend/src/components/__tests__/AnalysisPanel.annotation-gate-reload.test.tsx` (gate reload rendering). Extended: `frontend/src/utils/transformLoadResponse.test.ts`, `frontend/src/components/__tests__/AnalysisPanel.annotation-proportions.test.ts`.

- `0.33.12` — Snapshot V2 h5ad sidecar system, runtime-name validation, quota accounting rewrite, preflight runtime-choice enforcement; version bump (2026-07-15):
  - **h5ad sidecar system (`backend/utils/runtime_apply.py`, `backend/utils/sandbox.py`)**: Runtime capture now persists AnnData objects as standalone `.h5ad` files alongside the dill pkl, managed via `runtime_h5ad_manifest.json`. Sandbox restore rehydrates from both pkl and h5ad manifest with full name/path/hash validation. `quarantine_live_runtime()` covers h5ad sidecars. New `install_live_runtime_h5ad_set()` consolidates h5ad install + stale-cleanup. Restore/recovery paths use it consistently.
  - **Runtime-name validation (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/runtime_names.py`, `backend/services/snapshot_v2/models.py`)**: New `validate_runtime_global_name()` enforces safe Python identifiers (no path traversal, dots, spaces). Pydantic validators on `RuntimeDependency`, `RuntimeSkip`, `RuntimeDependencyManifest`, `RuntimeManifest` reject unsafe names at the model boundary, preventing path-injection in h5ad sidecar filenames.
  - **Quota accounting rewrite (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/service.py`)**: Replaced `register_existing_ref()`-based quota counting with a pre-capture snapshot of object-store hashes. New `_track_ref_with_result()` helper handles both `ObjectPutResult` (with `created` flag) and bare `ObjectRef` (counted only if hash absent before capture). Eliminates `register_existing_ref` returning True for newly-created-but-already-on-disk refs.
  - **Preflight runtime-choice enforcement (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/preflight.py`)**: `runtime_choice_supported` logic flipped: True only when runtime is NOT "complete" or has no payload. When restoring a restorable snapshot with `runtime_choice=None`, raises `RuntimeChoiceRequired` instead of silently accepting. Corrupted snapshots bypass enforcement.
  - **Planning stage validation order fix (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/continuation.py`)**: `planning_stage` check moved before `put_bytes()` so invalid stages fail closed without writing any objects to the object store.
  - **Live h5ad verification (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/live_verify.py`)**: `verify_live_restore_components()` now validates h5ad sidecar existence, SHA256 hashes, and manifest consistency after restore.
  - **Checkpoint path centralisation (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/checkpoint_paths.py`)**: New `checkpoint_sqlite_path()` helper used across service, restore, recovery, live_verify, and test files.
  - **New tests**: `test_snapshot_v2_runtime_names.py` (name validation), `test_snapshot_v2_runtime_h5ad_restore.py` (h5ad manifest/install/quarantine/recovery), `test_snapshot_v2_quota_accounting.py` (real capture-path physical_added_bytes), `test_snapshot_v2_checkpoint_paths.py` (checkpoint_sqlite_path contract). Extended: `test_snapshot_v2_continuation.py`, `test_snapshot_v2_preflight.py`, `test_snapshot_v2_restore_transaction.py`, `test_snapshot_v2_restore_acceptance.py`.

- `0.33.11` — Snapshot V2 robustness: planning_stage pre-validation, deletion lineage from live state, GC batch-count tolerance, corrupt-journal skip, huge-traceback collapse; version bump (2026-07-15):
  - **Planning stage pre-validation on capture (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/continuation.py`)**: `GraphNodeCapsule` construction now validates `planning_stage` live-descriptor values before persisting, surfacing an invalid value as a typed `SnapshotCaptureError` (symmetric with the restore-side check) instead of a raw pydantic `ValidationError`.
  - **Deletion lineage head from live state (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/deletion.py`)**: `previous_lineage_head` in the deletion journal now reads from the live in-memory `AnalysisState.last_snapshot_id` rather than the target snapshot's own ID, providing accurate pre-deletion lineage metadata. Visible manifest set is snapshot once and reused across surviving-objects and surviving-ids passes, avoiding redundant re-verification.
  - **GC import-batch count tolerance (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/gc.py`)**: GC batch counting now iterates `ImportBatchStore.list_batch_ids()` and skips unverifiable batches individually (`SnapshotIntegrityError` logged as warning) instead of failing the entire count. Exception logging added for observability without aborting GC.
  - **Corrupt journal skip in protected-ID loaders (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/exporter.py`, `backend/services/snapshot_v2/importer.py`, `backend/services/snapshot_v2/jobs.py`, `backend/services/snapshot_v2/service.py`)**: Removed narrow `(SnapshotIntegrityError, RecoveryFailure)` exception tuples that caused `NameError` when the referenced exception class was not imported at the call site — switched to bare `except Exception` so corrupt/incomplete journal entries are skipped cleanly.
  - **Legacy RuntimeGenerationManifest removal (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/runtime.py`)**: Removed dead transitional code that assembled and attached a `RuntimeGenerationManifest` on a private attribute — unused since the Snapshot V2 migration.
  - **Frontend huge-traceback collapse (`frontend/src/components/snapshots/SnapshotFailureDialog.tsx`)**: Multi-thousand-character kernel tracebacks are now split: the first line shown as a human summary, the full text tucked into a collapsible `<details>` disclosure. Dialog layout uses `max-h-[85vh]` with `overflow-y-auto` on the content area and `shrink-0` on the action footer, preventing the dialog from pushing action buttons off-screen.
  - **New tests (`backend/tests/test_snapshot_v2_export.py`)**: `test_load_active_export_protected_ids_skips_corrupt_journal` — regression test for the bare `except` fix. **Frontend (`frontend/src/components/__tests__/SnapshotDialogs.v2.test.tsx`)**: `collapses a huge traceback message behind a details disclosure while keeping actions reachable`.

- `0.33.10` — AnnData uns recursive sanitization (kernel template + host), ANSI error-strip in SnapshotCaptureError; version bump (2026-07-14):
  - **AnnData uns recursive sanitization (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/runtime.py`)**: Both the kernel template (`_SAVE_ANNDATA_TEMPLATE`) and the host-side `sanitize_anndata_for_h5ad()` now recursively walk `uns`, coercing unwritable leaves (bare `pd.Series`, `pd.Index`, `pd.Categorical`, formulaic ModelMatrix/ObjectProxy) to `ndarray` or dropping them. Previously `uns` was entirely left for AnnData's own writer, but objects such as PyDESeq2's `uns['trend_coeffs']` (a bare `pd.Series`) have no registered h5ad writer under `/uns` and caused `write_h5ad` to fail during runtime capture. Nested Scanpy metadata dicts (neighbors, pca, log1p, rank_genes_groups) are preserved.
  - **ANSI escape-strip in SnapshotCaptureError (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/runtime.py`)**: `_introspect_kernel_globals` and h5ad capture error paths now apply `strip_ansi()` from `backend.utils.error_parsing` to kernel error messages before wrapping them into `SnapshotCaptureError`, preventing raw terminal escape codes from surfacing in HITL dialogs and logs.
  - **New tests**: `backend/tests/test_snapshot_v2_runtime.py` (`test_sanitize_anndata_coerces_unwritable_uns_leaves`, template assertion updated to `_sanitize_uns_mapping`); `backend/tests/test_snapshot_v2_runtime_kernel.py` (`test_anndata_with_unwritable_uns_series_is_captured`).

- `0.33.9` — Frontend realtime messages de-persisted (server-authoritative); version bump (2026-07-14):
  - **Frontend realtime messages de-persisted (`frontend/src/stores/appStore.ts`)**: Removed `messages` from Zustand `partialize()` — realtime messages are now server-authoritative (kept in `AnalysisState.realtime_messages` on disk, re-hydrated from `/session/{id}/load` on open/refresh/reconnect). Previously persisted to localStorage with complex base64-stripping logic, risking stale or crash-prone shapes. The `merge()` migration now always `delete ps.messages` instead of conditionally dropping them, ensuring no legacy copy is ever trusted.
  - **New test (`frontend/src/stores/__tests__/appStore.persist.test.ts`)**: Added test "does not persist realtime messages" verifying `messages` are excluded from the persisted state while `chatMessages` and `currentSession` still survive.

- `0.33.8` — Version bump (2026-07-14): No code changes.

- `0.33.7` — PainterAgent unified protocol cascade with round-retry; version bump (2026-07-14):
  - **Unified protocol cascade (`backend/agents/painter_agent.py`)**: Replaced provider-specific branching (DashScope native / Chat modalities / Images API) with a single unified cascade that tries all three protocols in order regardless of provider. `base_url` only nudges which protocol goes first (DashScope→native, OpenRouter→chat, everything else→Images API). Fatal errors (401/403/content-moderation) abort immediately; all other failures (including timeout and parse errors) fall through to the next protocol. Full 5-round retry with exponential backoff (2/4/8/16s) when all protocols fail a round. New constants: `_MAX_RETRY_ROUNDS` (5), `_INTER_PROTOCOL_DELAY` (1s), `_PROTOCOL_TIMEOUT` (60s). Revision (`revise_graphic_abstract`) now uses a dedicated `_images_edit_attempt` (Images edits API `/v1/images/edits`) with automatic `response_format` fallback. `_extract_image_from_images_response` extracted as a shared helper for both generations and edits. Old `_retry_with_backoff` helper removed.
  - **Test rewrite (`backend/tests/test_painter_retry.py`)**: Replaced 3 old backoff-centric tests with 9 focused tests covering protocol ordering (default images-first, OpenRouter chat-first, DashScope native-first), cascade switching (first protocol success returns immediately, non-fatal failure switches to next protocol), fatal-error abort (401/403 aborts cascade), round-level retry with backoff (all 3 protocols fail round 1, images succeeds round 2 after 2s sleep), all-rounds exhaustion, per-protocol timeout switching, and revision edit protocol (falls back to chat when edits API fails).

- `0.33.6` — Snapshot V2 "Skip snapshot & continue" HITL action, soft-skip absent advisory runtime deps, runtime tracker "del" pruning; version bump (2026-07-13):
  - **Snapshot failure gate Skip action (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/failure_gate.py`, `backend/services/snapshot_v2/errors.py`, `backend/models/schemas.py`, `backend/routes/lifecycle.py`, `backend/workflow/core.py`)**: New `SNAPSHOT_FAILURE_ACTIONS_SKIPPABLE` tuple adds "skip" alongside retry/manage/terminate for producers where skipping is safe (workflow_boundary, post_report, chat). `skip_workflow_boundary()` / `skip_post_report()` / `skip_chat()` handlers arm a one-shot `skip_required_snapshot_phase` marker on `AnalysisState`; `consume_snapshot_skip()` in `_capture_required_snapshot()` makes the next required capture a no-op. `POST /{session_id}/snapshot-failure/skip` endpoint dispatches to the correct producer handler. pre_restore producer explicitly excludes skip (would overwrite live state without a safety point → 409).
  - **Soft-skip absent advisory runtime deps (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/runtime.py`)**: `RuntimeCapture.capture()` now distinguishes mandatory (checkpoint) vs advisory declared names. Absent advisory names are soft-skipped (recorded as incidental skips) instead of raising `required_runtime_unpersistable`, preventing transient/unused declared outputs from permanently bricking snapshot capture. Absent checkpoint dependencies still fail closed.
  - **Runtime dependency tracker "del" pruning (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/runtime_registry.py`, `backend/utils/sandbox.py`, `backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: `RuntimeDependencyTracker.record_execution()` accepts a new `removed` set — names deleted from the kernel namespace (e.g. a `loompy` connection) are pruned from the required and created sets, preventing permanently broken snapshots. Sandbox passes the `removed` names on code execution; bioinformatics agent derives `runtime_dependencies_v2` from the tracker as single source of truth instead of append-only accumulation.
  - **Frontend Skip button (`frontend/src/components/snapshots/SnapshotFailureDialog.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/AnalysisPanel.tsx`)**: New Skip button rendered when `allowed_actions` includes "skip" and producer is not pre_restore. AnalysisPanel gains `handleSnapshotFailureSkip` callback that POSTs to the skip endpoint and reconnects the WebSocket. `SnapshotFailureAction` type widened to include "skip".
  - **New tests**: `test_snapshot_failure_gate.py` (skippable producers offer skip, base actions exclude skip), `test_snapshot_v2_runtime.py` (absent advisory soft-skip, absent checkpoint still fails closed, removed global pruned from required), `test_snapshot_v2_capture_transaction.py` (embedding soft-skip regression update), `test_snapshot_v2_producers.py` (post_report skip action), `SnapshotDialogs.v2.test.tsx` (Skip button rendered/hidden), `SnapshotV2.acceptance.test.tsx` (Skip producer-gated acceptance).

- `0.33.5.3` — Frontend Snapshot V2 manifest shape adapter (flat-backend → wrapped-panel); version bump (2026-07-13):
  - **`normalizeSnapshotRecordV2` adapter (`frontend/src/utils/snapshotV2.ts`)**: New adapter that normalizes the backend's flat manifest dump (no `manifest` envelope, `continuation` instead of `capsule`, `created_at` instead of `captured_at`, `branch_id`/`branch_label` instead of `branch`) into the wrapped `SnapshotRecordV2` shape the panel/tree/preview consume. Defensively handles corrupt entries (missing manifest, error fields) to prevent crashing the render.
  - **`listSnapshotsV2` normalization wiring (`frontend/src/hooks/useApi.ts`)**: Response type widened to accept `unknown[]` raw snapshots; each entry mapped through `normalizeSnapshotRecordV2` before returning.
  - **New tests (`frontend/src/hooks/__tests__/useApi.snapshot-v2.test.ts`)**: 2 tests — real backend manifest shape (flat → wrapped) and corrupt entry without crashing manifest access.
  - **Regression test (`frontend/src/components/__tests__/SnapshotPanel.v2.test.tsx`)**: Verifies the panel renders adapter-normalized backend manifest shape without crashing into ErrorBoundary.

- `0.33.5.2` — Snapshot V2 quota check missing-v2-root regression fix; version bump (2026-07-13):
  - **Quota check missing-v2-root fix (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/service.py`)**: `_check_quota` now probes parent directories when `SnapshotV2Paths.root` does not exist, falling back to the nearest existing ancestor for `shutil.disk_usage`. Real captures never create the `v2/` root dir before the quota check, so `disk_usage` would raise `FileNotFoundError` and block the workflow at bioinformatics start.
  - **Regression test (`backend/tests/test_snapshot_v2_capture_transaction.py`)**: `test_check_quota_when_v2_root_dir_missing` reproduces the `DISK_USAGE_CHECK_FAILED` scenario using real `SnapshotV2Paths` (frozen dataclass, no ensure_dirs) with a missing `v2/` root, verifying `_check_quota` does not raise.

- `0.33.5.1` — Snapshot V2 ObjectStore lazy directory creation on first write; version bump (2026-07-13):
  - **Lazy staging/objects dir creation (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/object_store.py`)**: New `_ensure_store_dirs()` method called at the top of `put_bytes`, `put_file`, and `put_stream` to create missing `staging/` and `objects/` directories on demand. Fresh sessions only create workspace/states/uploads/reports/logs; Snapshot V2 dirs are created lazily so `put_*` never fails with `FileNotFoundError`.
  - **New tests (`backend/tests/test_snapshot_v2_object_store.py`)**: 3 tests (`test_put_bytes_creates_missing_staging_and_objects_dirs`, `test_put_file_creates_missing_staging_and_objects_dirs`, `test_put_stream_creates_missing_staging_and_objects_dirs`) validating that each `put_*` method creates the required directories when they do not exist.

- `0.33.5` — ErrorBoundary self-recovery (reset local data), Zustand persist migration, defensive array checks in MessageStream/TextInputDialog; version bump (2026-07-13):
  - **ErrorBoundary self-recovery (`frontend/src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx`)**: New "Reset local data" button that clears app-owned localStorage keys (`PERSIST_STORAGE_KEY`, `JWT_STORAGE_KEY`) and reloads, providing a recovery path when the app crashes on every load due to corrupted/legacy persisted state. Uses shared storage constants.
  - **Top-level ErrorBoundary wrapper (`frontend/src/main.tsx`)**: Wrapped the entire React app root with `<ErrorBoundary>`, providing crash containment at the top level instead of only inside individual components.
  - **Defensive array checks (`frontend/src/components/MessageStream.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/TextInputDialog.tsx`)**: Added `Array.isArray()` guards before `.length` checks on `content.findings`, `directions`, `qd.options`, `fd.findings`, `fd.suggestions`, and `suggestions` props, preventing rendering crashes when the backend returns truthy non-array values or null.
  - **Zustand persist migration (`frontend/src/stores/appStore.ts`)**: `migratePersistedState()` drops volatile runtime fields (`messages`, `chatMessages`, `currentSession`) on version upgrade (v0 → v1), preventing crashes from legacy-shaped persisted data. Persist config now uses `PERSIST_VERSION` (1) and `PERSIST_STORAGE_KEY` constants instead of raw strings.
  - **Frontend ErrorBoundary tests (`frontend/src/components/__tests__/ErrorBoundary.test.tsx`)**: New test verifying "Reset local data" clears both storage keys. `beforeAll` stubs `window.location.reload` for jsdom compatibility.

- `0.33.4` — Safe-point availability queuing, restore job safe-point reservation, frontend retry polling dedup & auto-open; version bump (2026-07-13):
  - **Safe-point availability queuing (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/safe_point.py`)**: New `request_safe_point_when_available()` with `_availability_waiters` registry — when a safe point is held by another owner, callers can wait with timeout instead of immediately failing. `release()` wakes the next waiter via `_notify_available_unlocked()`. `_request_safe_point_unlocked()` extracted as atomic helper. Prevents safe-point conflicts when multiple restore jobs or chat snapshots target the same session concurrently.
  - **Restore job safe-point reservation (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/jobs.py`)**: `enqueue()` now reserves the safe point via `request_safe_point_when_available()` before persisting the journal (5s timeout). On reservation failure, raises `SAFE_POINT_BUSY` (HTTP 409). Rollback on start failure cleans up registry, journal, and cancels reservation. `_run_job()` uses the pre-reserved `safe_point_request` instead of calling `request_safe_point()` at runtime. `RestoreJobHandle` gains `safe_point_request` field.
  - **`SAFE_POINT_BUSY` → HTTP 409 (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/errors.py`)**: `SnapshotTransactionError` with code `SAFE_POINT_BUSY` now returns 409 Conflict instead of 503, giving API callers a proper conflict signal for retry.
  - **Frontend retry polling dedup & auto-open (`frontend/src/components/AnalysisPanel.tsx`)**: Poll dedup via `transactionId` ref prevents duplicate concurrent polls. Stale poll guard stops when `snapshotTransaction` changes. New `useEffect` resumes polling hydrated non-terminal transactions on mount/reload. Auto-opens `SnapshotPanel` when a retry-of (`retry_of_transaction_id`) transaction is detected, improving UX for retried restore jobs.
  - **Frontend snapshot_transaction hydration (`frontend/src/hooks/useApi.ts`, `frontend/src/utils/transformLoadResponse.ts`, `frontend/src/types.ts`)**: `applyStoreUpdates()` and `transformLoadResponse()` now handle `snapshot_transaction` field end-to-end, preserving non-terminal transactions across page reloads.
  - **New tests**: `test_snapshot_v2_safe_point.py` (3 tests: waiter reservation, waiter timeout preserves owner, cancelled waiter cleanup), `test_snapshot_v2_restore_jobs.py` (3 tests: owned request before return, foreign pending rejection, second enqueue waits), `test_snapshot_v2_api.py` (SAFE_POINT_BUSY → 409 mapping), frontend `AnalysisPanel.snapshot-failure-retry.test.tsx` (3 tests: hydration resume, stale poll guard, auto-open on retry), `useApi.test.ts` (snapshot transaction hydration), `transformLoadResponse.test.ts` (snapshot transaction roundtrip).

- `0.33.3` — Snapshot V2 retry closure: pre-restore retry queues new job instead of inline capture, lifecycle route returns transaction metadata, safe-point active indicators in frontend (2026-07-13):
  - **Pre-restore retry rework (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/failure_gate.py`)**: Pre-restore retry now queues a new restore job linked to the terminal transaction instead of re-invoking capture inline. `retry_pre_restore()` clears the HITL gate and sets `PAUSED` status. New `retry_of_transaction_id` propagation across retry attempts.
  - **Restore job retry chain (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/jobs.py`)**: `RestoreJobManager.enqueue()` accepts `retry_of_transaction_id` parameter; status tracking preserves the retry chain across job records.
  - **Lifecycle response enrichment (`backend/routes/lifecycle.py`)**: `snapshot_failure_retry` endpoint returns `transaction_id` and `snapshot_transaction` fields for frontend display.
  - **Frontend retry UX (`frontend/src/components/AnalysisPanel.tsx`, `frontend/src/types/snapshotV2.ts`)**: Retry dialog shows transaction metadata; safe-point active indicators.

- `0.33.2` — Snapshot V2 safe-point generation tracking, import quota enforcement, chat snapshot safety (owner token + pre-snapshot status preservation), WORKFLOW_GRAPH_NODES registry, STOPPED task status, termination_reason, V1 export key isolation, batch storage (2026-07-13):
  - **Safe point generation tracking (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/safe_point.py`)**: Acknowledge/request/cancel all require owner token and generation parameter; prevents stale/overlapping safe-point holds across concurrent workflow/chat/restore paths.
  - **Import quota limits (`backend/config/_config.py`)**: 6 new `snapshot_v2_import_*` config knobs (compressed/uncompressed/member/manifest/snapshot count caps) enforce per-import resource bounds.
  - **Graph node registry (`backend/constants.py`)**: New `WORKFLOW_GRAPH_NODES` frozenset as authoritative single source of truth, replacing inline node lists across workflow construction and routing.
  - **AnalysisState extensions (`backend/models/schemas.py`)**: New `STOPPED` task status; `restore_provenance_snapshot_id`, `restore_start_intent`, `restore_resume_workflow_state`, `runtime_restore_warning`, `termination_reason` fields for durable restore provenance tracking and audit logging.
  - **Chat snapshot safety (`backend/routes/chat.py`)**: Safe-point owner token mechanism prevents cross-path conflicts; pre-snapshot status preserved for failure recovery; chat blocked during active snapshot/restore; centralized `enter_chat_snapshot_failure_gate()` for consistent HITL on snapshot failure.
  - **V1 export key isolation (`backend/routes/export_import.py`)**: Export prepared state keyed as `v1-{session_id}` to prevent collision with V2 export job IDs.
  - **Snapshot V2 module enhancements**: `batch_store.py` (batch storage), `deletion.py` (safe-point-aware deletion), `exporter.py` (V2 export with safe-point fencing), `importer.py` (quota enforcement), `dispatcher.py` (generation-aware dispatch), `object_store.py` (shutdown awareness), `recovery.py` (generation tracking), `runtime.py`/`workspace.py` (safe-point fencing).
  - **Frontend snapshot V2 UX (`frontend/src/components/AnalysisPanel.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/SnapshotPanel.tsx`, `frontend/src/stores/appStore.ts`, `frontend/src/utils/snapshotV2.ts`)**: Transaction status display, restore progress with safe-point awareness, new utility helpers.
  - **25+ new/modified test files**: Lifecycle, export, import, GC, continuation, idempotency, pre-restore, security, runtime, workspace, and V1 cutover coverage.

- `0.33.1` — Snapshot V2 content-addressed lifecycle: fail-closed runtime capture, restore reset/keep semantics, import trust_runtime=false, V1 restore 410 cutover, continuation capsules, integrity verification, storage quota governance, per-session safe points; persistent_outputs tool field; bioinformatics safe-point integration; AGENTS.md/CLAUDE.md/README.md Snapshot V2 documentation (2026-07-12):
  - **Snapshot V2 module (`backend/services/snapshot_v2/`)**: 25 new files (10,710 lines) implementing a ground-up rewrite of the snapshot system — content-addressed object store (`objects/`), staging/committed lifecycle, journals for restore/delete, integrity material, safe-point descriptors, pre-restore validation, runtime capture/restore, GC, import/export, and multi-phase continuation capsules. Service layer (`service.py`, 2,082 lines) orchestrates capture → verify → commit with transactional staging.
  - **Fail-closed runtime capture**: When runtime exceeds quota or serialization fails, workflow pauses at HITL (Retry / Manage Snapshots / Terminate) — **no Skip**. `@with_retry` in `retry.py` routes `SnapshotCaptureError` to the V2 HITL handler with distinct action set.
  - **Restore reset/keep semantics**: `reset` quarantines runtime and forces re-run from inputs; `keep` retains current runtime with persistent risk acceptance. Import `trust_runtime=false` never materializes runtime objects from archives.
  - **V1 cutover**: V1 restore returns HTTP 410. V1 inventory/export/delete remain for read-only management. Frontend: `LegacySnapshotInventory`, `RestoreProgressDialog`, `RuntimeChoiceDialog`, `RuntimeWarningBanner`, `SnapshotFailureDialog`, `SnapshotPreview`, `SnapshotTree`, `StorageSummary`.
  - **Integrity & storage governance**: Host-side `integrity/` material, `SNAPSHOT_V2_*` quota constants, `snapshot_v2_max_runtime_mb` / `snapshot_v2_integrity_verification_enabled` / `snapshot_v2_fail_closed_enabled` config knobs.
  - **Route refactors**: `POST /v2/capture`, `POST /v2/restore`, `GET /v2/inventory`, `POST /v2/import`, `GET /v2/export/{id}`. Session lifecycle routes check for active V2 staging before interrupting captures.
  - **persistent_outputs tool field (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: New `persistent_outputs` field in `execute_code` tool schema (required) for namespace variable declarations critical to snapshot restore correctness.
  - **Bioinformatics safe-point integration**: Safe-point request/pause/resume coordination in the bioinformatics agent step loop for snapshot-safe code execution.
  - **Doc updates**: AGENTS.md Snapshot V2 lifecycle section, CLAUDE.md Snapshot V2 patterns section, README.md Snapshot V2 safety contract table.
  - **74+ new test files** across backend unit/integration and frontend Vitest suites, covering capture/restore transactions, fault matrix, portability, real-kernel value cycle, security, concurrency, journals, deletion, GC, export, import, pre-restore, continuation, runtime kernel, structural boundary, storage acceptance, and V1 cutover.
  - **AnnData alias disclaimer**: Snapshot V2 does not guarantee Python object alias identity when the same AnnData object is embedded inside arbitrary nested containers (`nested["adata"] is adata` may not hold after restore).

**0.32.x — (2026-07-03)**

- `0.32.12` — Integration gate auto-skip → PI decide, centralised round serialization, frontend restore error display (2026-07-09): 
  - _The changes originally intended for `0.32.12` were merged into the `0.33.1` Snapshot V2 release; this entry exists for version continuity._

- `0.32.11` — Stable integration gate detection via analysisPhase, `analysisPhase` type widening (2026-07-09):
  - **Stable integration gate detection (`frontend/src/components/AnalysisPanel.tsx`)**: Extracted `isCurrentIntegrationGate` combining both `status === 'waiting_input'` and `analysisPhase === 'integration'`. The effect that opens the `IntegrationGatePanel` now depends only on this stable boolean instead of `currentSession?.status`, preventing spurious re-triggers. When not in integration gate, `integrationGateData` is reliably cleared (functional setState). `IntegrationGatePanel` renders only when `isCurrentIntegrationGate && integrationGateData`, and `RoundFeedbackDialog` excludes it with an additional render-level guard.
  - **`analysisPhase` type widening (`frontend/src/stores/appStore.ts`, `frontend/src/types.ts`)**: Added `'integration'` to the `analysisPhase` / `analysis_phase` union type, preventing TypeScript errors when the backend reports integration-phase sessions.

- `0.32.11.1` — Sidebar default session name i18n, session defaults extraction (2026-07-09):
  - **Session defaults extraction (`frontend/src/constants/sessionDefaults.ts`)**: New module with `DEFAULT_SESSION_NAME` and `DEFAULT_SESSION_INSTRUCTION` constants, shared between Sidebar and NewSessionForm.
  - **Sidebar default session name i18n (`frontend/src/components/Sidebar.tsx`)**: Default session name now derived from i18n translation key instead of hardcoded string.

- `0.32.10` — Integration gate robustness, frontend dialog guard for integration rounds (2026-07-07):
  - **Integration gate continue robustness (`backend/workflow/synthesis.py`)**: A generic continue signal (e.g. `/human-input` with no direction or any route that bypassed `/choose-direction`) at the integration gate now defaults to `PI_DECIDE_SIGNAL` instead of leaving the gate unresolved. Previously the graph would proceed to `initialize_round` with no integration direction selected; now the gate always resolves by delegating to PI.
  - **Frontend integration gate dialog guard (`frontend/src/components/AnalysisPanel.tsx`)**: The generic `RoundFeedbackDialog` is never opened during integration-phase rounds — the dedicated `IntegrationGatePanel` handles those. Added render-level guard (`!integrationGateData`) to defend against stale state on reconnect/rehydrate. The `feedbackRoundData.phase !== 'integration'` check prevents accidental dialog display when integration rounds share the same round number as a prior innovation round.

- `0.32.9` — Snapshot restore validation hardening, direction dict type widening, PI JSON array parsing fix (2026-07-07):
  - **Snapshot restore safety (`backend/routes/snapshots.py`)**: Wrapped restore body in `try/finally` guaranteeing `_initializing_sessions` cleared on every exit path (including early 422 and late failures). Added `_format_restore_error()` helper producing human-readable `detail` strings. Catches `SnapshotRestoreError`/`ValidationError` → HTTP 422 with the formatted detail.
  - **State normalization + fail-closed validation (`backend/utils/snapshot.py`)**: `normalize_direction_dicts()` coerces `is_custom` (→bool) and `candidate_index` (→int) before `model_validate`. New `SnapshotRestoreError` exception class. `_validate_state_with_fallback()` blanks only allowlisted drift-tolerant fields (`integration_cycle`) but fails closed on all others. `RECOVERABLE_RESTORE_DEFAULT_FIELDS` frozenset documents which fields may be safely defaulted.
  - **Direction dict type widening (`backend/models/schemas.py`)**: `user_chosen_directions`, `directions_queue`, `active_direction_batch` changed from `List[Dict[str, str]]` to `List[Dict[str, Any]]` to accommodate `is_custom: bool` and `candidate_index: int|None` without silent coercion.
  - **PI JSON array parsing fix (`backend/agents/pi_agent.py`)**: `generate_integration_direction_candidates()` and `score_integration_directions_with_evidence()` switched from `_safe_parse_json()` (which silently drops top-level arrays) to `safe_parse_json_array()`.
  - **Frontend restore error display (`frontend/src/components/SnapshotPanel.tsx`)**: Added `restoreError` state; inline error banner in the confirmation dialog; clears on cancel.
  - **Frontend test setup (`frontend/src/test-setup.ts`)**: Added `ResizeObserver` stub preventing `ReferenceError` in component tests.
  - **New tests (`backend/tests/test_pi_score_field_contract.py`)**: 2 tests verifying integration direction candidate generation and scoring parse JSON arrays correctly.

- `0.32.8` — Centralized integration report staging for detailed report appendix (2026-07-06):
  - **Shared integration report staging (`backend/services/report/download_section.py`)**: New `build_related_reports_metadata()` function copies the integration report HTML from its source path into the target reports/branch directory, ensuring branch-specific detailed reports include the integration report. Handles path resolution, `shutil.copy2` (skipped when source and target are the same file), and OSError logging.
  - **Consistent consumption (`backend/agents/supervisor_agent.py`, `backend/services/report/generator.py`)**: Both `SupervisorAgent._build_appendix()` and `DetailedReportGenerator._build_appendix_data()` now call `build_related_reports_metadata()` instead of constructing `related_reports` inline, eliminating the stale/hardcoded `integration_report` path that broke when reports lived in branch directories.
  - **Regenerator cleanup (`backend/services/report/regenerator.py`)**: Removed a duplicate `integration_report` fallback assignment in `_build_appendix_from_report()` that was made redundant by the new centralized helper. Fixed missing trailing newline.
  - **New tests (`backend/tests/test_appendix_literature_link.py`, `backend/tests/test_report_appendix_integration_report.py`)**: Added 3 tests verifying: integration report link comes from `related_reports` dict; top-level `integration_report` key is ignored by regenerator; `build_related_reports_metadata` copies the integration report into a branch output directory on request.

- `0.32.7` — IntegrationGatePanel i18n, plan_review wait_seconds alignment, ResultsPanel integration summary (2026-07-05):
  - **IntegrationGatePanel i18n (`frontend/src/components/IntegrationGatePanel.tsx`, `frontend/src/i18n/translations.ts`)**: Replaced hardcoded Chinese text with bilingual (zh/en) text driven by `language` prop. Title, countdown label, and all three action buttons follow the app language setting. New i18n keys: `integrationGateTitle`, `integrationGateCountdown`, `integrationGateContinue`, `integrationGateLetPiDecide`, `integrationGateFinalize`. Test updated (`IntegrationGate.test.tsx`) — new test covers English text rendering.
  - **Plan review wait_seconds alignment (`backend/workflow/planning.py`, `frontend/src/components/AnalysisPanel.tsx`)**: `plan_review` WebSocket message now includes the resolved `wait_seconds` from `resolve_plan_review_wait()`. Frontend uses `planReviewData.wait_seconds` as the countdown source when present, falling back to `roundWaitSeconds`. Previously the plan review countdown always used `roundWaitSeconds`, which diverged from the backend timeout when `auto_approve_plans` was enabled with a different `plan_review_wait_seconds`.
  - **ResultsPanel integration summary (`frontend/src/components/ResultsPanel.tsx`)**: Integration report summary rendered in the ResultsPanel round card (matching the existing display in AnalysisPanel), providing consistent UX across both UI panels.

- `0.32.6` — Integration round fixes: WS message type registration, PI_DECIDE routing bypass, round serialization, phase-qualified keys, report summary surface (2026-07-05):
  - **WS message type registration (`backend/models/schemas.py`)**: `integration_gate` and `integration_complete` added to `WebSocketMessage.type` Literal. Previously missing, causing `ValidationError` outside `messaging.py`'s try/except and crashing `_integration_synthesis` under `@with_retry`, overrunning `integration_cycle`. New guard test (`test_websocket_message_types.py`) validates every `_send_message` type in workflow/ is registered.
  - **Integration-gate PI_DECIDE routing bypass (`backend/workflow/direction_coverage.py`, `backend/workflow/routing.py`)**: New `awaiting_integration_direction()` predicate detects when auto-skip set `PI_DECIDE_SIGNAL` with `integration_cycle > 0`. `_should_continue` honors it in the integration-loop guard, so PI direction selection can reach `pi_planning` past `max_rounds`. The integration cycle cap still stops the loop in `_check_convergence_condition`. New test file (`test_integration_pi_decide_routing.py`, 3 tests).
  - **Centralized round serialization (`backend/routes/serialization.py` — new)**: `serialize_round_results()` preserves `phase`, `integration_candidates`, and `integration_report_summary` for all round results. Used by both `websockets.py` (completed-session reconnect) and `reports.py` (report-load), replacing duplicated inline serialization that dropped integration fields. New test file (`test_round_serialization.py`).
  - **Phase-qualified round keys (`frontend/src/utils/roundKey.ts` — new)**: `roundKey(phase, round)` returns `"int-N"` for integration rounds and `"r-N"` for exploration/innovation, preventing React key collisions and reload de-dup issues when integration rounds reuse the same round number as the previous innovation round. AnalysisPanel's `expandedRound` state type changed from `number` to `string`; ResultsPanel's `expandedRounds` Set changed from `Set<number>` to `Set<string>`. WS `round_summary` handler de-dups by `roundKey` instead of round number. New test file (`roundKey.test.ts`, 4 tests).
  - **Surface integration report summary + candidate scores (`frontend/src/types.ts`, `frontend/src/stores/appStore.ts`, `frontend/src/utils/transformLoadResponse.ts`, `frontend/src/components/AnalysisPanel.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/ResultsPanel.tsx`)**: `integration_report_summary` mapped end-to-end (raw type → store → transform → component) and rendered in integration round cards. ResultsPanel now shows candidate scores matching AnalysisPanel. Updated WS test (`useApi.ws.test.ts`) verifies integration round stays separate from same-numbered innovation round.
  - **Design spec**: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-05-fix-integration-round-issues.md` (911 lines) documents all 5 issues, reproduction paths, and implementation steps.

- `0.32.5` — Integration gate auto-skip → PI decide, integration RoundResult, graph short-circuit for zero direction, shared round-summary payload, frontend integration round display (2026-07-05):
  - **Integration gate auto-skip now triggers PI_DECIDE (`backend/workflow/synthesis.py`)**: `_wait_for_user_input` integration-gate timeout no longer sets `final_report_requested`. Instead, it sets `user_chosen_direction = PI_DECIDE_SIGNAL` and switches phase to `INNOVATION`, giving PI a chance to select directions from integration candidates. User explicit finalize (via `/finalize-from-integration` API) continues to set `final_report_requested` directly.
  - **Integration gate accepts let_pi_decide (`backend/routes/interaction.py`)**: `_choose_direction_locked` now allows `let_pi_decide=True` at the integration gate, switching to `INNOVATION` phase instead of rejecting. Test updated: `test_integration_gate_accepts_let_pi_decide` replaces the old rejection test.
  - **Integration round RoundResult creation (`backend/workflow/integration.py`)**: `_integration_synthesis` now creates a `RoundResult` with `phase="integration"`, `integration_report_summary`, and `integration_candidates`. Round summary WebSocket messages (`round_summary`) include `phase` and `integration_candidates` fields. Integration detailed segments now pass the integration `RoundResult` properly.
  - **Graph short-circuit for zero integration directions (`backend/workflow/graph.py`, `backend/workflow/routing.py`)**: New conditional edge `_after_pi_planning` routes to `generate_final_report` when PI selects 0 integration directions (via `final_report_requested`), skipping the agent pipeline. New `_after_pi_planning()` method in `RoutingMixin`.
  - **Shared round-summary payload builder (`backend/workflow/synthesis.py`)**: `build_round_summary_payload()` extracted from `_pi_synthesis` into a standalone helper, shared between regular rounds and integration rounds. Payload now consistently includes `phase` and `integration_candidates`.
  - **Integration gate PI_DECIDE routing in pi_planning (`backend/workflow/planning.py`)**: `_pi_planning` distinguishes integration-gate PI_DECIDE (via `integration_cycle > 0`) from exploration-gate PI_DECIDE. PI selects from `integration_gate_candidates` via `select_integration_directions()`. When PI selects 0 directions, sets `final_report_requested=True` and returns empty instructions with skip_nodes to short-circuit. PI agent gains `select_integration_directions()` method.
  - **Integration gate PI selection agent method (`backend/agents/pi_agent.py`)**: New `select_integration_directions()` — PI evaluates scored candidates from integration analysis and selects genuinely novel, evidence-supported directions. Unlike `select_best_directions`, it does NOT force at least 1 direction; empty selection is a valid outcome.
  - **`RoundResult` schema extended (`backend/models/schemas.py`)**: `RoundResult.phase` now allows `"integration"` in addition to `"exploration"` and `"innovation"`. New fields `integration_report_summary` and `integration_candidates` store integration-phase data. `_serialize_round_results()` includes `phase`, `integration_report_summary`, and `integration_candidates`.
  - **Frontend integration round display (`frontend/src/components/AnalysisPanel.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/ResultsPanel.tsx`)**: Integration rounds render with purple styling, labeled "Integration N" ("整合轮 N"), with candidate research directions listed in a purple-themed card. `IntegrationGatePanel` gains "Let PI decide" button and countdown timer with auto-trigger. No-wait mode (`countdownSeconds=0`) triggers PI decide immediately.
  - **Frontend type plumbing (`frontend/src/types.ts`, `frontend/src/stores/appStore.ts`, `frontend/src/hooks/useApi.ts`, `frontend/src/utils/transformLoadResponse.ts`)**: `RoundSummary` and `RoundResult` gain `phase` and `integrationCandidates` fields. WebSocket `round_summary` handler, store persistence, and load-response transformer all support the new fields. `GitMerge` icon imported.
  - **New test files (4 files)**: `test_integration_synthesis_node.py` (integration round result creation, round summary message). Updated `test_integration_endpoints.py` (let_pi_decide accepted at integration gate), `IntegrationGate.test.tsx` (PI decide button, countdown timer prop).
  - **New untracked test files (2)**: `test_integration_gate_autoskip.py`, `test_integration_round_result.py`.

- `0.32.4` — Plan review wait aligned with round-wait semantics (2026-07-05):
  - **Plan review wait unified with round_wait_seconds (`backend/workflow/planning.py`)**: `resolve_plan_review_wait()` now inherits `round_wait_seconds` semantics instead of always returning `-1` when `auto_approve_plans` is `False`. Plan review now follows the same auto-skip (0) / countdown (>0) / manual (-1) behavior as all other HITL gates. Timeout while waiting for plan review action now always auto-skips instead of sending a status message, matching the round-wait pattern.
  - **Tests restructured (`backend/tests/test_plan_review_wait.py`)**: 6 focused tests replace 4 ambiguous ones — each test case covers one unambiguous semantic: round_wait=0 auto-skips, positive round_wait applies to plan review, manual (-1) blocks plan review, auto_approve_plans uses dedicated plan_review_wait_seconds, auto_approve + zero seconds auto-skips.

- `0.32.3` — Code deduplication refactoring: prompt blocks, gate predicates, WS payload builders & frontend selection reset (2026-07-04):
  - **Shared prompt-fragment builder (`backend/agents/prompt_blocks.py`)**: New `build_integration_block()` with three styles (`final`/`integrated`/`detailed`) replaces 3× duplicated integration-reports inlining across PI and Supervisor agents. PI prompts (`backend/agents/pi_agent.py`) and Supervisor report prompt (`backend/agents/supervisor_agent.py`) both consume it via `from .prompt_blocks import build_integration_block`.
  - **Shared download-section HTML renderer (`backend/services/report/download_section.py`)**: New `render_related_reports_downloads_html()` provides a single renderer for the "Related Reports & Downloads" appendix block, consumed by SupervisorAgent appendix (`backend/agents/supervisor_agent.py`), report regenerator (`backend/services/report/regenerator.py`), and report sections (`backend/services/report/sections.py`).
  - **Shared direction-record entry formula (`backend/services/report/interaction_records.py`)**: Extracted `_direction_record_entry_pairs()` and `direction_record_entries()` as single-source-of-truth for direction-record filename/label computation. Both `write_interaction_record_files()` and the downloads renderer consume the same formula. Early-exit guard prevents `mkdir` when nothing to write.
  - **Shared capture helpers (`backend/services/interaction_record_capture.py`)**: New `populate_direction_record_from_state()` and `resolve_integration_gate()` deduplicate direction-record construction across `_choose_direction_locked` and `finalize_from_integration` route handlers (`backend/routes/interaction.py`). `resolve_integration_gate()` also clears `integration_gate_candidates`/`literature_evidence` as a single atomic operation.
  - **Shared integration-gate payload builder (`backend/workflow/helpers.py`)**: `build_integration_gate_payload()` replaces 3× inlined payload construction across `core.py` (reconnect send), `integration.py`, and `snapshots.py` (snapshot restore re-send). Consumed via `from .helpers import build_integration_gate_payload`.
  - **Shared integration-gate predicates (`backend/workflow/direction_coverage.py`)**: `at_integration_gate()`, `active_batch_incomplete()`, `active_batch_complete()` replace duplicated batch-coverage and phase+candidate checks across `core.py`, `routing.py`, `synthesis.py`, `reporting.py`, and `websockets.py`. `_multi_direction_analysis_completed()` uses `active_batch_complete()`. `validate_complete_report_allowed()` uses `active_batch_incomplete()`.
  - **Gate string constants (`backend/constants.py`)**: New `GATE_ROUND_WAIT`, `GATE_DIRECTION_SELECTION`, `GATE_INTEGRATION_SELECTION`, `GATE_ANNOTATION_CONFIRMATION`, and `SEMANTIC_WAIT_GATES` frozenset replace raw string literals across `core.py`, `synthesis.py`, `lifecycle.py`, `restore_targets.py`. `SNAPSHOT_RESUME_TARGET` in `restore_targets.py` uses the constants.
  - **`consume_final_report_request()` method (`backend/models/schemas.py`)**: `AnalysisState.consume_final_report_request()` provides an atomic read-and-clear of the one-shot `final_report_requested` flag, replacing manual `=False` assignments in `core.py` (report-only reconnect path) and `synthesis.py` (integration finalize path).
  - **`_check_convergence_condition` simplification (`backend/workflow/routing.py`)**: Uses shared predicates from `direction_coverage.py` for all batch/coverage checks. Eliminated duplicated `max_rounds` fallback logic — now delegates to `_should_continue()` via `return self._should_continue(state)` instead of reimplementing max-rounds checking.
  - **`_in_integration_loop` WS guard optimization (`backend/routes/websockets.py`)**: Cheap boolean early-return checks (`pending_integration`, `phase == INTEGRATION`, `integration_gate_candidates`) before O(N) batch scan via `active_batch_incomplete()`.
